  • How this draft was made

Tauheed, Bab 7 «رؤیت»: hub plan

28 lessons from 16 Fasls. Every lesson opens and closes: tap a title. Inside, The steps is open; the rest is folded. Source refs are hidden; the Refs button shows them. Comment by selecting text, or with a section's button.

new taught here first · ↺ B3 S04 partly taught there; the new part is taught here · ↺ B1 S03 taught there; one-line reminder here · core repeat · ask kept in full, you decide · ask for you · Claude's judgement an import kept on Claude's framing

Gate D: for Ali to approve

Each item has my pick. Per-lesson Asks stay in each entry; Ali answers them on Reading Mode.

  1. 28 lessons from 16 Fasls. The diagnosis splits ten Fasls in two and U7.12 in three; U7.14, U7.18, U7.21 and U7.24 stay one lesson each, and U7.2 is one short lesson. Fold test (a fold where recalls empty a lesson): no lesson is emptied by recalls, because Bab 7's own attribute teaching (knowledge, power, will, speech, wisdom, mercy) is mostly new to Bab 1 to 6 (points: SAME 24, PART 133, NONE 394). Fallbacks: 26 (S03 into S01, U7.42's S25 and S26 into U7.41's S23 and S24) or 29 (S11 split after "nothing tires Him"; S23 split at its jud step). Recommended: 28.
  2. Verdicts the readers changed after reading both passages (each an Ask in its entry): H.80 PART to NONE (S11: EZ reads the Kursi as the Imam's stations, no power claim); O.131 PART to NONE (S28); NONE to PART: J.9 (S17, the mi'raj in Ali's voice, B5 S07 and B6 S06 TC), O.10 (S24, B1 S07), O.60 and O.84 (S25, B4 S01, B3 S05), O.89 (S26, B3 S19). Repeats the readers found outside the packets, each an Ask: S10's core base in B2 S18 (Ask 24), S12 H.68 and H.14 in B4 S08 TC (Ask 26), S20 EZ P016 in B1 S18, S27 step 3 in B1 S18 (Ask 3), S25 the dua «إلهي ضيفك ببابك» in B1 S18 (Ask 18). Recommended: accept.
  3. The AH re-read finding. 154 of the 216 verified AH re-read records are SAME (already written in Bab 1 to 6), 26 PART, 36 NONE; 34 are off the Bab's subject. As Bab 6 found, every Bab re-reads the same AH sessions, so most records are recalled in one line with the repeat under the recall as an Ask. Consequence: several lessons have little or no AH or MM in their new teaching (S09, S12, S13, S15, S16, S21, S24, S26 above all); each entry says so plainly and asks. The Teacher Copy may carry EZ and JS (2026-11-18). Recommended: accept; the AH and MM share is a Handout matter for later.
  4. Imports on Claude's judgement (thin lessons only, bottom quarter by AH/MM share, P25 51.6%: S10, S16, S18, S21, S25, S27, S28; S18 has none, no later Fasl fits His speech). 169 rows judged: 31 kept (S10 4, S16 6, S21 3, S25 1, S27 15, S28 2), 138 dropped with the reason. S27's kept rows rest on the atlas lines only (no import passage gathered yet) and two quote no AH words; many U10.11 and all U10.13 rows carry no AH or MM text in their gathers. Every kept row is Teacher Copy only with a CLAUDE'S JUDGEMENT (not the scholar's words) line; S28's two are kept only if AH's passage, once gathered, says it. Recommended: accept the kept rows; read S28's two.
  5. Arabic printed again (86 texts across the Bab, each flagged with the earlier lesson). The vowel-free table gave 238 shared texts; the readers kept only real reprints, set aside short shared runs, and found reprints the table missed (S20, S22, S23). Many reprints depend on a Teacher Copy choice and are counted only if that item stays. Recommended: writers rerun the vowel-free search per lesson (BAB-LESSONS 26).
  6. Material held out of the hub (22 ledger points: Imamat, Mahdaviyat, Nubuwwat, and M2/M3 Fasls of this Bab) and the off-subject AH re-read records (34: Imamat, Mahdaviyat, hudud, khums): they stay in the Teacher Copy, flagged, not moved. Recommended: keep as held; their place is decided with the Imamat and Mahdaviyat courses.
  7. Sensitive AH remarks found by the re-read (each an Ask where it sits): "God too needs money" on khums (RR5-027, clashes with ghina), the poem «خدا را حول و قوت جز علی نیست» (RR3-039), the reading of «لا حول و لا قوة الا بالله» (ZH-S26), tawassul and bada claims, harsh polemic asides. Recommended: Teacher Copy only, flagged, never Handout.
  8. Texts. 698 narration-like texts checked in Jami (FOUND 528, OTHER-WORDING 119, NOT-FOUND 22, OTHER-SPEAKER 19, POEM 10), each placed by lesson in open-questions/UNVERIFIED-TEXTS-B7.md; the Arabic review (373 items) is on Reading Mode. Known cuts that reverse the sense («ما» dropped at U7.42-3 P082:9 and U7.41-3 P121:4-5) and restores that matched a commentary (U7.24-2 P115, U7.30-1 P045, U7.42-3 P082) are Asks. Recommended: writers print Jami's wording with the Ask.
  9. Long lessons. S11 (48 points, 41 Asks) and S23 (47 points) fold much material. Recommended: keep; split only if the written lesson runs long (fallback 29).
  10. The M2 and later Fasls of B07 (27 Fasls) are not built. Recommended: later, after M1 is approved, as in Bab 5 and Bab 6.
  11. Undertone: none, as Bab 5 and 6. Recommended: none.

The 28 lessons

اللہ ہر کمال کا مالک ہے: وہ غنی، کریم اور عزیز ہے؛ اس کا علم ہر چیز کو گھیرے ہوئے ہے اور پہلے سے جانتا ہے مگر مجبور نہیں کرتا؛ اس کی قدرت کی کوئی حد نہیں؛ وہ ارادہ کرتا اور کلام کرتا ہے اور یہ اس کا فعل ہے؛ وہ حکیم ہے اور اس کی رحمت اس کے غضب پر سبقت رکھتی ہے؛ اسی لیے بندہ اسے پکارتا، اس پر بھروسہ کرتا اور اس کی حمد و شکر کرتا ہے۔

1. Every perfection is His; the signs and the takbir S01 to S03

S01 «اللہ ہر عیب سے پاک اور ہر کمال کا مالک ہے، اور اس کے کمالات کی کوئی انتہا نہیں»

ledger H01 · 3 new · 9 part-taught before · 3 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Which God do we mean? The One who introduces Himself by His names (King, Living, Knowing, Powerful, Holy, Lord of power and grandeur, Owner of being, knowledge and life), and whom fitra already finds: near, answering, beyond thought. (MM P001:1, 4, 30-31; EZ P004:5 with 112:1-2 inline, one line; JS P003:29-30 one ayah block, 59:23; AH P040:1-19, JS P041:5-8 under the recall; B7.A.1, B7.A.18). [A.1 PART: recall B1 S12 "the Quran introduces God by its own verses and al-Ikhlas" and B2 S09 "the heart finds a living, knowing, powerful Reality", then new: God presenting Himself by the names Living, Knowing, Powerful as the very content of the attributes, in MM P001:4, 30-31 | A.18 SAME: recall only, "Recall B2 S09: fitra finds a near, knowing, powerful, living Reality without proof and without grasping the Essence"]
  2. Who then deserves worship? Only the One who is not Himself held up by another: "you cannot keep yourself, how will you keep others?"; worship belongs to the God Moses introduced, present and absent, near and far, Living and Self-subsisting. (AH P002:1-8; JS P020:5-12 «وجدتک أهلاً للعبادة» inline; B7.A.1 C1). [PART (part of A.1): recall B4 S17 "Asiya's answer, used for steadfast tawhid", then new: AH's reading that only the One not held up by another is fit for worship, in AH P002:1-8; RR1-028 (fire worship) is SAME and sits under the recall]
  3. Is there any defect in Him? None: His attributes are of being, not of non-being; no lack, no defect, no awaited state; all else, from the Seal of the Prophets to the dust in the air, is deficient and needy in itself. (EZ P047:3-10 lead-in, one line; AH P005:1-4; JS P006:8-16 one line; B7.A.47, B7.A.3). [A.3 core repeat kept in full (see Repeats); new only AH's awaited state, P005:1-2 | A.47 PART: recall B3 S04 "al-Sadiq to the zindiq: I did not limit Him but affirmed Him", then new: EZ's "the God of the prophets has attributes of being, not of non-being" against nihilism, in EZ P047:3-10 (Teacher Copy layer) | RR1-023, RR3-007, RR2-027 SAME: recalled under this step]
  4. Why must every perfection be His? He is the source of perfections: being, knowledge, power and life issue from Him; all perfections are His and from Him. (EZ P008:4; JS P017:9-13 one line; part of B7.A.3). [part of the A.3 core repeat | RR3-003 (a giver cannot lack what it gives) SAME: recalled, so AH carries no new line here; see Ask 6]
  5. How far do His perfections go? Without end, as His Essence is without end: knowledge before any known thing, power before any object of power, and no end to knowledge, power, generosity, mercy or love; beyond the endless, and that beyond-ness is itself endless. (MM P009:1 first clause, P009:5; MM P011:2-3; AH P013:21-28, P010:1-4; MM P030:1 one line; RR5-026 one line; B7.A.4, B7.A.10). [A.4 PART: recall B4 S13 "free of every defect", B3 S07 "His power infinite beside a limited creation" and B1 S18 "His infinite perfection", then new: knowledge, power and life each stated as endless, in MM P009:1, P011:2-3 and AH P013:21-28 | A.10 PART (Teacher Copy): recall B1 S05 "there cannot be two infinites" and B3 S07, then new: endlessness is His alone, the world of light is only called infinite, in MM P030:1 | RR5-026 NEW: AH, paradise endless "as long as God's godhood", one line]
  6. Can our reason reach that end? No: multiply billions by billions and reason still says "more"; whatever we picture of His attributes and acts reaches no end. (AH P022:2-23; AH P014:8-9; MM P021:1-2 one line; JS P024:6-9 fine layer; B7.A.5). [A.5 PART: recall B1 S05 and B4 S11 "intellects are bewildered in Him", then new: bewilderment at the endlessness of His perfections, the billions example, in AH P022:2-23]
  7. Can He then be described? He is greater than to be described; His is the highest description, above any description of the Prophet, the angels or anything great. (JS P016:7 block «اللَّهُ أَکْبَرُ مِنْ أَنْ یُوصَفَ» with P016:3-6; JS P045:21-24 and P031:2-5 with 16:60 printed once, 30:27 inline; JS P082:3-5 Teacher Copy; B7.A.25, B7.A.26). [A.25 NEW | A.26 NEW (Teacher Copy, same reading as A.25, Kanz al-Daqa'iq's Arabic gloss out) | RR3-020 SAME: recalled here]
  8. What is the least we must know of Him? That He is ever Knowing, Hearing, Seeing, and does what He wills. (JS P044:3-15; B7.A.23). ↺ B4 S01recall only, "Recall B4 S01: the least knowledge of the Creator, «لم يزل عالماً وسامعاً وبصيراً وهو الفعال لما يريد»"; so the lesson lands on step 7, see Ask 8
Asks for Ali 24
  1. Split U7.1 into S01 (perfection and endlessness) and S02 (our share and His greatness)? Recommended yes (diag U7.1 Q1).
  2. B7.A.3 core repeat kept in full (taught in B4 S13, B5 S02): which Bab carries it in full? Recommended Bab 7 (Repeats).
  3. Step 1 keeps one ayah of the JS P003 run (59:23); the other blocks and the inline list (2:163, 3:26, 6:102, 42:11, 57:1-6, 59:22, 59:24) to the Teacher Copy or out? Recommended Teacher Copy, list only (diag section 5).
  4. B7.A.18 SAME: AH P040, JS P041, MM P001:43-44 sit under the recall; teach again? Recommended no (Repeats).
  5. B7.A.23 SAME and B7.A.21 SAME: JS's repeats sit under their recalls; teach again? Recommended no.
  6. RR1-023, RR1-028, RR2-027, RR3-003, RR3-007, RR3-020 (SAME): each sits under its recall; teach any again? Recommended no. With RR3-003 recalled, step 4 is EZ and JS lines only: fold step 4 into step 3? Recommended fold.
  7. The re-read record AZ-S11 ("no mithl, but mathal"), proposed by the diagnosis for step 7, is not placed in S01: place it here so AH carries step 7? (diag Q22)
  8. With B7.A.23 SAME, step 8 is a recall only: land the lesson on step 7 («الله أكبر من أن يوصف», 16:60) and keep the Kafi report as the recall line? Recommended yes.
  9. B7.A.5 is Teacher Copy in the ledger but carries step 6: raise AH P022:2-23 to the Handout? Recommended yes (diag chain A6).
  10. 2:255 (JS P024:8) is the Bab's first full print in S01's Teacher Copy, while the ledger (B7.A.22) prints it once in H27: print it in H27 only and keep S01 at the one line «ولا یؤوده حفظهما»? Recommended yes.
  11. RR5-026 (NONE, paradise endless as God's godhood) as one AH line in step 5? Recommended yes, Teacher Copy if Ali finds it off the point.
  12. AH P005:3 «صادرِ اول اور حجابِ اعظم» for the Prophet: keep the wording beside "only God is unlimited"? (diag Q4)
  13. AH P075:1 is AH's own supplication, and «عرفت الله بجمعه بين الأضداد» (P076:10) is in Jami only as an unattributed saying (Jami' al-Sa'adat 1:174), not a Masum's: teacher note only, not as a narration? Recommended yes (diag Q14, Q15; ledger ASK on B7.A.19).
  14. EZ P092, God's Essence as «نور، ظاهر لنفسه ومظهر لغيره» (MM uses the definition for the light of reason): out of the Handout, teacher note at most? Recommended yes (diag Q3; ledger ASK on B7.A.53).
  15. JS P088 (Nahj 1) held for U7.6 so that S01 stays purely affirmative? Recommended yes (diag Q5; ledger ASK on B7.A.29).
  16. B7.A.21, A.51, A.52 held for U7.9 (M2)? Recommended yes (ledger ASKs). If kept as teacher notes: restore the Friday du'a from the Sahifa (Jami: «الحمد لله الأول قبل الإنشاء والإحياء والآخر بعد فناء الأشياء العليم الذي لا ينسى من ذكره»; the recording's «رب قبل الإنشاء», «الی ینسى» are not in Jami) and cite al-Irshad p. 94 for al-Husayn to Zaynab, not 'Awalim with empty volume and page (diag Q19).

Next lesson picks up

if every perfection is His, whose are the perfections we see in ourselves, and what is left for us before Him? «اگر ہر کمال اسی کا ہے تو ہم میں جو کمال نظر آتا ہے وہ کس کا ہے، اور اس کے سامنے ہمارا حصہ کیا ہے؟» (S02)

S02 «ہمارا ہر کمال اسی کا عطا کیا ہوا سایہ ہے، اور اس کی بے انتہا عظمت کا حق کوئی ادا نہیں کر سکتا»

ledger H02 · 2 new · 7 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Whose are the perfections we have? His are His own, from Himself, borrowed from no one («خودآ»); ours are His shadow and gift: "O rich one, you are rich through Me; O knower, you know through Me; O parents, your love is a shadow of My love"; no one can call his share his own apart from Him. (AH P035:3-7, P025:2-8; EZ P018:1-9 the finger example; JS P027:1-8 with «لا حول ولا قوة إلا بالله» inline; B7.A.8). [core repeat kept in full (see Repeats); new only the examples: "through Me you are rich", parents' love, EZ's finger | RR1-006, RR1-008 SAME: recalled under this step]
  2. How does His endless gift reach a finite creature? Not all at once: the Karun river does not go into a jug; so it comes by degrees, the nearest first and through it the next; and our own share is little. (AH P074:12-30 jug example, technical terms out; one line that He gives by choice, not like perfume (EZ P008:5-9 is the question, U7.24 the answer); EZ P046:4-7 with 17:85 one line; B7.A.16, B7.A.44). [A.16 NEW | A.44 PART: recall B1 S07 "Majlisi's needle's eye for how little of His knowledge appeared to the Prophet (MM)" and B2 S10 "17:85, you were given but little knowledge (EZ)", then new: the creature's knowledge as its little share of His, in EZ P046:1-7 (the bird's beak and needle tip carry H08) | RR3-008 SAME: recalled under this step]
  3. Where do we see the shadow most clearly? In a mother's love: one sample in the shop window of what fills His unseen storehouse, beyond count in number and in depth. (AH P028:1-9, short; the full treatment and JS P029 «أرحم من کل رحیم» go to H23; B7.A.9). ↺ B2 S05Already taught: B2 S05 "EZ's dove and its chick: God loves us more than this mother",New here: no creaturely kindness reaches His mercy, in AH P028:1-9
  4. Did creating add anything to Him? Nothing: He was and nothing was with Him; He gave being, and gained no attribute He did not have. (EZ P090:1-8 with «کان اللّٰه و لم یکن معه شیء» after the reference is re-pointed; MM 7.4.7 al-Baqir "He did not grow strong after creating", if fetched; EZ P048 Ibn Arabi out of the Handout; B7.A.49, B7.A.46). [A.49 PART: recall B5 S02 "Nahj 186: He did not bring things into being to strengthen His rule or to increase His kingdom (JS)", then new: the plain claim that creating added nothing to Him, in EZ P090:1-8 (and MM 7.4.7) | A.46 PART (Teacher Copy): recall B6 S06 "EZ on 20:114: the other side is infinite, so the knowledge He gives never stops", then new: before He created there was nothing, in EZ P090:1-8 | RR4-004 SAME: recalled under this step]
  5. How did the Imams teach His greatness? "When the angels saw the height of our station we said takbir, so that the angels would know God is too great for any high station to be reached except through Him"; and every takbir, tasbih, tahmid and tamjid proclaims this. (MM P036:3 block, the takbir clause only, with MM's reading P036:3; MM P069:1, 3 one line each; B7.A.15). ↺ B1 S07Already taught: B1 S07 "the angels learned tasbih, tahlil and praise from the lights of the Fourteen (MM)",New here: MM's reading of the takbir as "too great for any station to be reached except through Him", in MM P036:3
  6. What did the Prophet see of it? In the mi'raj, a greatness at which his soul left him, and the ruku' tasbih came from it. (MM P073:4-5, ruku' clause only; B7.A.14). ↺ B6 S07 · B1 S07recall only, "Recall B6 S07 (and B1 S07): in the mi'raj he looked at the Throne, saw a greatness at which his soul left him, and was inspired to say «سبحان ربي العظيم وبحمده»"; see Ask 6
  7. Can anyone fulfil His due? No: His dominance is beyond the endless, so whoever thinks he has done His due, even at the highest rank, has fallen short; respect follows the rank of the one respected, and His rank is endless, so the friends of God confess shortfall and ask pardon even for their worship. (AH P037:4-24; EZ P038:5, 13-15, one graded-respect example P038:8-12, P038:70-81; B7.A.17). ↺ B1 S07 · B1 S17Already taught: B1 S07 "angels and the people of Paradise confess: we have not worshipped You as You deserve (MM)" and B1 S17 "even the Prophet stays on the way (JS)",New here: AH's ground in His endless dominance with the warning against self-regard (P037:4-24), and EZ's finite worship asking pardon for itself (P038:70-81) | RR2-037, RR3-021 SAME: recalled under this step
  8. Close: "My God, enough honour for me that I am Your servant, enough pride that You are my Lord; You are as I love, so make me as You love." (JS P087:3-15; B7.A.28; B7.A.54 under its recall). [A.28 PART: recall B5 S04 "the same munajat line, read inline in the Teacher Copy with no claim drawn (JS)", then new: read here as the creature's answer to His perfections, in JS P087:6-15 | A.54 SAME: recall only, "Recall B1 S12: Ali's travel du'a, You are the companion in travel and the deputy over the family, and only God joins both"]

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «فَلَمَّا شَاهَدُوا كِبَرَ مَحَلِّنَا كَبَّرْنَا لِتَعْلَمَ الْمَلَائِكَةُ أَنَّ اللَّهَ أَكْبَرُ مِنْ أَنْ يُنَالَ عِظَمُ الْمَحَلِّ إِلَّا بِهِ» (MM P036:3): FOUND, Bihar 26:336-337, Mustadrak Safina 8:216; MM names no speaker, the refiner added «پیغمبر اکرم»; the matn speaks in the first person of the mi'raj («لما عرج بي»), so the Prophet is likely, but confirm the speaker in Bihar 26 before naming him.
  2. «وَلا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللهِ» (JS P027:6, inline): a common formula (al-Mahasin 1:111-112, Mustadrak Safina 2:260); the lesson ties it to one du'a in Iqbal al-A'mal: drop the block and the du'a reference, keep it inline.
  3. ﴿وَمَا أُوتِيتُمْ مِنَ الْعِلْمِ إِلَّا قَلِيلًا﴾ (17:85, EZ P046:4-7, inline).
  4. «كان الله ولم يكن معه شيء» (EZ P090:6): Jami gives it as al-Sadiq's saying only in al-Fusul al-Muhimma 154 (late); the al-Kafi report (al-Baqir) reads «كان الله ولا شيء غيره»; the match printed in the part (Tafsir al-Safi 3:288) is al-Kashani's commentary, not a narration: re-point to al-Kafi's wording.
  5. The ruku' clause of the mi'raj report (MM P073:4-5): FOUND, 'Ilal al-Shara'i' (al-Sadiq's mi'raj report), Bihar 18:358-359; flagged reprint, B6 S07.
  6. «إلهي كفى بي عزا أن أكون لك عبدا وكفى بي فخرا أن تكون لي ربا، أنت كما أحب فاجعلني كما تحب» (JS P087, inline): FOUND, Kanz al-Fawa'id 1 («إلهي أنت لي كما أحب فوفقني لما تحب»), Masadir Nahj al-Balagha 130-131 («أنت كما أحب فاجعلني كما تحب», Amir al-Mu'minin's munajat): the attribution stands, so the «علی» of P087:11 is not an ASR slip to worry about.
  7. «أنت الصاحب في السفر ... ولا يجمعهما غيرك» (EZ P093:28): Nahj (Abduh 1) «وأنت الخليفة في الأهل»; the part prints A'lam al-Din 1:396 «والخليفة في الحضر» and a stray «»»: use the Nahj wording; flagged reprint, B1 S12.
Next lesson picks up

if our every perfection is His gift, how do we come to know His attributes at all: by signs, by reason, or by His own word? «جب ہمارا ہر کمال اسی کی عطا ہے تو ہم اس کی صفات کو کیسے پہچانیں: نشانیوں سے، عقل سے یا اس کے اپنے کلام سے؟» (S03)

S03 خدا کی صفات اس کی نشانیوں سے، اور سب سے قریب خود ہمارے اندر کی نشانیوں سے، پہچانی جاتی ہیں؛ مگر نشانیاں انہیں بے حد نہیں دکھاتیں، اس لیے عقل کا اصول اور وحی اس پہچان کو پورا کرتے ہیں، اور وحی خود سکھاتی ہے کہ وہ ہر وصف سے بڑا ہے

ledger · 8 new · 8 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. If He is not seen, how is He known? By proofs and signs: «معروف بالدلالات منعوت بالعلامات»; the heavens, the earth and the turning of night and day are signs for those with minds (3:190); from them we learn that He is not ignorant and not powerless (JS U7.2-1 P004:1-3, P004:6-8; B7.A.7, first half). ↺ B1 S11 · B3 S17Already taught: B1 S11 and B3 S17 "the work shows its maker; the giver of a perfection is not devoid of it"New here: the signs read off for the attributes, knowing, powerful, living. The Nahj 179 words are printed again, flagged (Arabic line below)
  2. What exactly do the signs tell? That their Maker knows, can and lives, as a book of precious, well ordered content shows that its author was wise and able; created things are His signs both in their being and in the qualities of their making (JS P007:1-2 first three sentences, P005:4-8 one line, P003:2-5; B7.A.7). ↺ B1 S11 · B3 S17Already taught: B1 S11 "EZ's watch and car show their maker" and B3 S17 "a precise watch shows its maker knew"New here: JS's book example read for the attributes, in P007:2. AH's world as a book (RR3-005) is SAME and sits under the recall
  3. Where is the nearest sign? In ourselves: the light of knowledge given to man is itself a sign; from the sign of his knowledge he knows that his Sustainer knows, from his power that He is powerful, from his life that He lives; a scholar's knowledge is a sample of His boundless knowledge, brought to the shop of this world, and He is «واحد لا بتأويل عدد»; so with His power, might, richness and mercy (MM P001:1 first and last sentences only; AH P002:1-7; B7.A.7). ↺ Bab 1 to 6Already taught: same recall as step 2,New here: MM's sign in the self and AH's sample, taught in full. AH's mithl and mathal (RR4-001) is SAME and sits under the recall
  4. Is this knowing complete? No: this road does not show that His knowledge and power are unlimited; He is not measured against people and not reached by the senses; His power is plain in creation, but the how of it is not something we grasp, and His power has no how at all (JS P007:2 the limit sentence, P004:9-11; pulled JS U7.21-4 P193:5-10; B7.A.7 new part, B7.H.30). [A.7 PART, new part: JS's limit; H.30 NEW]
  5. Then what completes it? Reason's principle that every perfection is His and every defect far from Him, one bridge line back to S01 where it is taught, and revelation, which confirms what reason knows in outline and goes beyond it (JS U7.2-2 P028:1-9, P030:1-7; B7.A.41, B7.A.43; P029, the concept of the Necessary, as one Teacher Copy line, B7.A.42). [A.41 PART: recall B5 S02 "every deficiency negated, every perfection affirmed (JS)" then new: the rule as the middle term for proving the attributes; A.43 NEW; A.42 PART, Teacher Copy: recall B2 S08 and B4 S12 "kalam proves a necessary being, then its attributes; JS accepts the term wajib al-wujud" then new: unpacking the concept as a road, P029:1-2]
  6. How does revelation itself describe Him? First by negating what does not befit Him, then by affirming: «لا يبلغ مدحته القائلون ولا يحصي نعماءه العادون» before «فطر الخلائق بقدرته ونشر الرياح برحمته»; and with praise He alone deserves: «اللهم أنت أهل الوصف الجميل والتعداد الكثير» (JS U7.2-1 P016:6-10, EZ P017:12 one clause of corroboration, JS P015:18 one line; B7.A.33, B7.A.34). [A.33 PART: recall B1 S02 and B5 S06 "Nahj sermon 1 up to 'the perfection of sincerity is negating attributes'" then new: JS's reading of the sermon's method, negation then affirmation; A.34 PART: recall B6 S02 and B3 S04 "«المعروف من غير رؤية» and sermon 91 on His signs" then new: JS reads sermons 90 and 91 as lists of attributes, one line]
  7. How many attributes can one phrase hold? «اللہ اکبر» falls on His eternity, knowledge, strength, power, forbearance, generosity, giving and grandeur, because He is greater than to be described; and the word «اللہ» gathers every attribute of perfection, which the Quran then lays out in a row, as in 2:255 (JS P009:2-9 with block P009:5, P018:1-14 one sentence; B7.A.11, B7.A.30). [A.11 PART: recall B5 S04 "«اللہ اکبر» means greater than to be described (the Kafi dialogue; MM's «أجل من أن يدرك الواصفون» printed there in full)" then new: one phrase holds many attributes, P009:5 and JS's gloss P009:6-8; A.30 NEW. The letters hadith as one Teacher Copy line, «فالعين من العلم والغين من الغنى» (A.35 PART: recall B4 S06 "the letters hadith, lam-alif is la ilaha illa Allah (JS)" then new: babs 32 and 33 read for the attributes)]
  8. Close: so the student describes God as the texts describe Him, knowing that he cannot describe Him as He is (JS P015:17 landing). [NEW as landing; the same thought closes B5 S04, one recall word]

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «لم تره العيون بمشاهدة العيان ولكن رأته القلوب بحقائق الإيمان ... معروف بالدلالات منعوت بالعلامات لا يقاس بالناس ولا يدرك بالحواس» (Amir al-Mu'minin to Dhi'lib; Nahj 179, Bihar 4:32; Jami FOUND-OTHER-WORDING for the printed P004:1, «مشاهده الایان ... یا عقول» is garbled and the speaker is left as «حضرت»; Jami order «لا يدرك بالحواس ولا يقاس بالناس»).
  2. «إن في خلق السماوات والأرض واختلاف الليل والنهار لآيات لأولي الألباب» (3:190, inline, JS P004:3).
  3. «واحد لا بتأويل عدد» (AH P002:4, no speaker named; Jami: Imam al-Rida's tawhid sermon, A'lam al-Din 69-70).
  4. «الحمد لله الذي لا يبلغ مدحته القائلون ولا يحصي نعماءه العادون ... فطر الخلائق بقدرته ونشر الرياح برحمته» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj sermon 1; Jami FOUND; Jami «نعماءه», the lesson «نعماه»).
  5. «اللهم أنت أهل الوصف الجميل والتعداد الكثير» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj 91, the Ashbah sermon; Jami FOUND; the lesson's heading says sermon 90).
  6. «فلقوله الله أكبر معان كثيرة منها أن قول المؤذن الله أكبر يقع على قدمه وأزليته وأبديته وعلمه وقوته وقدرته وحلمه وكرمه وجوده وعطائه وكبريائه» (Amir al-Mu'minin, through Imam Musa Kazim; Bihar 81:131, Ma'ani al-Akhbar, al-Tawhid, Falah al-Sa'il; Jami FOUND).
  7. «الله لا إله إلا هو الحي القيوم» (2:255, inline, JS P018:10-14; the "greatest ayah" claim of P018:15 has no source).
Next lesson picks up

جو خدا ہر کمال رکھتا ہے اور کسی چیز کا محتاج نہیں، اس نے مخلوق کو کیوں پیدا کیا، اور کیا ہماری عبادت اسے کچھ دیتی ہے؟ (S04)

2. Rich, generous, mighty S04 to S05

S04 اللہ ہر حاجت سے بے نیاز ہے: اس نے نہ کسی حاجت سے پیدا کیا، نہ عبادت اسے کچھ دیتی ہے اور نہ عطا اس کا کچھ گھٹاتی ہے؛ پھر بھی وہ اپنے محتاج بندوں سے یوں کریمانہ برتاؤ کرتا ہے گویا اسے ان کی حاجت ہو

ledger · 7 new · 7 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 2 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Did God create the world because He needed it? No: not from need, not to gain rank, not from loneliness, not for profit; He alone is perfect and every other is a beggar at His door, so He created to give, not to take; He is rich by His Essence, not by acquiring (MM U7.3-1 P001:2-4, re-cut from the Jami matn to «لا لحاجة كانت منه إلى ذلك ... ولا رأى لنفسه فيما خلق زيادة ولا نقصانا»; JS P014:2-8 cut to «لم تخلق الخلق لوحشة ولا استعملتهم لمنفعة»; AH P003:1-23; JS P013:5-14 «غني لا باستفادة»; JS P004:6-17 and P005:4-10 one line; B7.B.1, B7.B.3). [B.1 PART, kept in full as core repeat: recall B5 S02 "God was and nothing with Him; no need of any kind causes His creating (JS)" and B1 S01 "He created so that His grace reaches the contingents (AH)" then the new tellings, al-Rida to 'Imran (MM) and AH's beggars and Rumi line; B.3 PART: recall B5 S02 "Nahj 186, not to strengthen His rule, not from loneliness (block printed there)" then new: «غني لا باستفادة» and Nahj 109 «لم تخلق الخلق لوحشة»]
  2. Then why did He create and command? To test which of them is best in deed; He needs no helpers: «كونوا أنصار الله» is a test, not a need (MM P006:9 the one sentence «من غير فاقة بي إليك وإليهم وإنما خلقتك وخلقتهم لأبلوك وأبلوهم»; EZ P009:5-10 with 61:14; B7.B.2). ↺ B5 S02Already taught: B5 S02 "God needs nothing from His servants, no jihad, khums or zakat (JS, EZ, Teacher Copy there)"New here: MM's report and EZ on 61:14
  3. Does worship give Him anything, or sin take anything away? No: if all jinn and men worshipped day and night His station would not grow by a needle's point; He created them free of need of their obedience and safe from their disobedience; «فإن تكفروا أنتم ومن في الأرض جميعا فإن الله لغني حميد»; whoever turns back harms himself, not God; the servant's plea, my punishment adds nothing to Your kingdom (AH P016:1-9, P016:23-26 one line; JS P020:5-6; JS P028:2-5 with 14:8 in Tanzil wording; EZ P045:2-3 with 3:144; JS P022:12-16; B7.B.4). ↺ B1 S01 · B5 S02Already taught: B1 S01 "AH: knowing and using the sun adds nothing to it; if all disbelieved, no dust would settle on His grandeur" and B5 S02 "AH: our worship adds nothing to Him, «بر دامن کبریاش ننشیند گرد»"New here: Nahj 193, 14:8 as Fatima quoted it, 3:144, the Sahifa plea; AH's sun (P016:10-22) is the shared base, see Ask 4
  4. Then why worship and remember Him? Because we need it: His commands come from one who needs nothing, so every command is for our good, and the one most obedient to his Lord is the best well-wisher of his own self; the benefits of acts of worship are their fruits, not their reason; servanthood answers His call («إلهي هذه صلاتي صليتها لا لحاجة منك إليها»); and His remembrance honours those who remember: He is subbuh whether I glorify Him or not (JS P036:2-22; EZ P037:1-14 with one example, 2:183, P037:19-20, P037:22-24; EZ P040:27-31; JS P039:3-10 in one sentence, no classroom; JS P048:3-7; EZ P049:6-18; B7.B.4 sections 3 and 4, B7.B.5). [B.4 unpointed sections NEW (no Bab 1 to 6 match was looked for: they carry no point id, see Ask 5); B.5 PART: recall B1 S01 "worship adds nothing to Him (AH)" then new: «يا من ذكره شرف للذاكرين» (JS) and EZ's subbuh]
  5. Who, then, is rich? He alone; everything else is poor to Him, and our poverty toward Him is one to boast of; at the end of a report God Himself calls out «أنا الغني الذي لا أفتقر» (EZ P053:4-7 with the 35:15 block; JS P051:1-2; JS P052:25-29, with a one-sentence setting; B7.B.6; B.7 as fine layer: ghani as absolute self-sufficiency, hamid as acts praised for wisdom, JS P066:4-5, 10-20 with 22:64). [B.6 PART, kept in full as core repeat: recall B4 S06 and B3 S08 "35:15, creatures are pure need and He alone is rich" and B4 S10 "You the Rich, I the poor (EZ)" then new: «أنا الغني الذي لا أفتقر» and EZ's poverty to boast of; B.7 PART, Teacher Copy: recall B4 S12 "all depend on Him, He on none (JS)" then new: hamid as acts praised for wisdom. AH's four re-read records on continuous dependence are SAME and sit under the recall here]
  6. Does giving lessen Him? No: withholding does not enrich Him and giving does not impoverish Him; He is no more generous with what is asked than with what is not; no asker occupies Him and no gift diminishes Him (JS U7.3-2 P074:5-12, P073:4-20 with the block «فليس بما سئل أجود منه بما لم يسأل»; P075:14 block «لا يشغله سائل ولا ينقصه نائل»; P076:14 one line in Jami wording; B7.B.8, B7.B.9 as fine layer). [B.8 PART: recall B1 S16 "giving only increases His generosity (AH)" then new: Nahj 91 and the Ashbah lines (JS); B.9 NEW, Teacher Copy]
  7. Being so free of need, how does He deal with His servants? Nobly: He speaks of His favour only to make its worth known, as a father who puts a diamond on his child's finger, and the favour is the Prophet; He asks a loan of His servants; He buys from them what is already His, as a father buys his child's toy to turn him to his lessons; He shows them love as if He needed them (AH U7.3-1 P064:1-26, P064:36-50, one telling, 2:264 and 3:164; EZ U7.3-2 P069:4-20, P069:25-28, 2:245 once; EZ U7.3-3 P097:3-21 with 9:111; EZ P096:16-21 with the block P096:20; B7.B.23, B7.O.55, B7.B.28, B7.B.27). [all four NEW]
  8. Close: so the servant asks, let me not be needy in Your richness (JS U7.3-2 P079:2-4 block; B7.B.10). new

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «... لا لحاجة كانت منه إلى ذلك ... ولا رأى لنفسه فيما خلق زيادة ولا نقصانا» (Imam al-Rida to 'Imran al-Sabi; 'Uyun, Bihar 10:311-312; Jami FOUND; the clause is in the Jami matn, past the two excerpts printed in the part; the lesson prose has «نقصا»).
  2. «غني لا باستفادة» (Amir al-Mu'minin; Nahj, Bihar 74:310 «فاعل لا باضطراب آلة مقدر لا بجول فكرة غني لا باستفادة»; Jami FOUND; the block was left verified=no; the speaker was inferred, now confirmed).
  3. «فإن الله سبحانه خلق الخلق حين خلقهم غنيا عن طاعتهم آمنا من معصيتهم» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj 193, the Hammam sermon; Jami FOUND; EZ's attribution of «غنيا عن طاعتهم» to Hazrat Zahra, P037:21, is not found).
  4. «يا أيها الناس أنتم الفقراء إلى الله والله هو الغني الحميد» (35:15, block, EZ P053:6).
  5. «فليس بما سئل أجود منه بما لم يسأل» with «لا يفره المنع والجمود» (Amir al-Mu'minin, the Ashbah sermon; al-Tawhid, Bihar 4:274-275, Nahj 91; Jami FOUND; the Nahj text with «بأجود» not found).
  6. «كأن بك الحاجة إليهم وأنت يا سيدي الغني عنهم» (Imam Sajjad; Bihar 84:201, al-Manaqib of Ibn Shahrashub; Jami FOUND).
  7. «اللهم إني أعوذ بك أن أفتقر في غناك أو أضل في هداك» (Amir al-Mu'minin; Nahj, Subhi 588, Muhaj al-Da'awat; Jami FOUND).
Next lesson picks up

جو ہر چیز سے بے نیاز ہے، کیا عزت، کبریائی اور عظمت بھی صرف اسی کی ہیں، اور بندہ ان کے سامنے کیا کرے؟ (S05)

S05 عزت اور کبریائی صرف اللہ کی ہیں؛ بندے کی عزت اسی کی عطا ہے، اور بندہ اس عظمت کے سامنے جھک کر ہی بلند ہوتا ہے

ledger · 6 new · 4 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. اللہ کی عظمت کا مطلب کیا ہے؟ The sum of all His attributes, beauty and majesty together; He is «عظیم», not «اعظم», since nothing stands beside Him to be compared (U7.3-3 JS P085:5-6, 18-32, classroom frame dropped; B7.B.17). [NEW; one reconciling line with «الله أكبر من أن يوصف» (H03) per Q14]
  2. عزت اور کبریاء کیا ہیں؟ Might is the state that cannot be overcome, grandeur is to be above submission; in that sense both are His alone, and the two are two aspects of one greatness (U7.3-1 JS P054:2-3, P059:4-7, 13-17 in Urdu with no book named, P061:3-8 with 45:37; U7.3-2 JS P081:3-10, 14-21 fine layer; B7.B.12 definitions, B7.B.13). [PART (B7.B.12, core repeat, see step 3): the definitions and 45:37 are new; B7.B.13 NEW, Teacher Copy]
  3. کیا کوئی اور یہ صفات اپنا سکتا ہے؟ No: He wore them, chose them for Himself above His creation, made them His sanctuary and cursed whoever contends with Him for them; «الكبرياء ردائي» (U7.3-1 JS P055:4-5 Qasi'a block cut to «... من عباده», P056:1-12; MM tells the same sermon, U7.1-1 MM P034:4, first clauses only; U7.3-1 EZ P057:4-6, «الكبرياء ردائي» once; even the angels address Him as «العزيز الجبار القاهر», U7.21-3 MM P187:3-6, one line, B7.H.23). [CORE REPEAT B7.B.12, kept in full: recall B4 S07 "only God may say 'I'; grandeur is His and whoever claims it is crushed" then taught; PART B7.A.12: recall B4 S07 for AH's khud-a argument, new: MM's Qasi'a telling; B7.H.23 NEW]
  4. تو مؤمن کی عزت کہاں سے ہے؟ From God's grant: «ولله العزة ولرسوله وللمؤمنين» (63:8, once); al-Husayn to the man who said «إن فيك كبرا»: «كل الكبر لله وحده», and the might in me is God's gift (U7.3-1 JS P059:8-11; EZ P060:7-14, no book named). [PART within B7.B.12: new part, taught in full in EZ's and JS's wording]
  5. کیا بندہ اللہ کی ہر صفت اپنے اندر لا سکتا ہے؟ No: we do not give our attributes to Him, and we were never told to become the mirror of all His attributes; grandeur, samadiyya and absolute knowledge stay His, man gets at most a likeness and stays bound; khayla' (self-importance) is from tajabbur (U7.3-1 EZ P065:7-29, P058:1; U7.3-3 EZ P094:1-9, khayla' block in Tuhaf's wording). [PART B7.B.24: recall B5 S02 / B6 S03 "no creaturely mark may be ascribed to Him" then new: EZ's answer to the questioner, P065:13-29; PART B7.B.25: recall B4 S07 / B1 S17 "pride is His; a grain of pride felled Satan" then new: EZ against takhalluq, khayla' as tajabbur, P094:5-9]
  6. تو بندہ اس عظمت کے سامنے کیا کرے اور کیا مانگے؟ Not grandeur for himself: he humbles himself to the truth, and God raises him not by the measure of his humility but by the measure of His own greatness; before His subduing might even death is a tajalli of His qahhariyya and the grave a barzakhi one (U7.3-2 JS P080:1-13 block, P080:10-12; U7.21-3 AH P190:1, 39:42 inline; B7.B.11, B7.H.27; Dua al-Sahar only if Ali answers Q9, B7.B.26 Teacher Copy). [B7.B.11 NEW; B7.H.27 NEW (the Imam Hasan line is the RR4-009 report: recall); B7.B.26 NEW, Teacher Copy; SAME B7.H.41 recall B3 S07; SAME RR1-009, RR4-009 recalled]
  7. Close: all things glorify Him because He is the Mighty and the Wise: His might puts all under His rule, His wisdom leaves no flaw in His act (U7.3-2 JS P082:1-8, 57:1 inline; 57:2 and P082:9-15 move to H11). [PART B7.B.14: recall B3 S04 / B5 S05 "all things glorify Him, by the tongue of state and of speech" then new: they glorify Him because He is Mighty and Wise, P082:1-7]

Qasi'a «الْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ الَّذِي لَبِسَ الْعِزَّ وَالْكِبْرِيَاءَ وَاخْتَارَهُمَا لِنَفْسِهِ دُونَ خَلْقِهِ ...» (Nahj, Subhi 1:116; Jami FOUND, store Mustadrak Safina 3:91-92; told three times in the Fasl, printed once). «الكبرياء ردائي» (hadith qudsi; Jami FOUND, Tafsir al-Safi 5:10 «الكبرياء ردائي والعظمة إزاري ...»). 63:8 «وَلِلَّهِ الْعِزَّةُ وَلِرَسُولِهِ وَلِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ» and 45:37 «وَلَهُ الْكِبْرِيَاءُ فِي السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ» (inline). «كل الكبر لله وحده» (al-Husayn; Jami FOUND, Mustadrak Safina 9:8; the lesson cites Kanz al-'Ummal, a Sunni book). «وَاعْلَمْ أَنَّ اللَّهَ لَمْ يَرْفَعِ الْمُتَوَاضِعِينَ بِقَدْرِ تَوَاضُعِهِمْ وَلَكِنْ رَفَعَهُمْ بِقَدْرِ عَظَمَتِهِ» (al-Kazim to Hisham; Jami FOUND, Tuhaf al-'Uqul 399; lesson ref Bihar 75:314). «إِنَّ الْخُيَلَاءَ مِنَ التَّجَبُّرِ ...» (Amir al-Mu'minin; Jami FOUND-OTHER-WORDING: Tuhaf al-'Uqul 203 «إن الخيلاء من التجبر والتجبر من النخوة والنخوة من التكبر ...»; the printed cut drops «إن الخيلاء»). «ربنا أنت العزيز الجبار القاهر العظيم الشأن» (angels; Jami FOUND, Bihar 54:324-325; MM ref Qisas al-Rawandi). 57:1 (close).

Next lesson picks up

اللہ کے عالم ہونے کی دلیل کیا ہے، اور یہ کیسا جاننا ہے؟ (S06)

3. His knowledge S06 to S10

S06 جس نے پیدا کیا وہی جانتا ہے: خالق ہونا ہی اللہ کے عالم ہونے کی دلیل ہے

ledger · 1 new · 2 part-taught before · 3 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 6
  1. جب اللہ کا عالم ہونا بدیہی ہے تو اس کی دلیل کیوں پوچھیں؟ It is obvious, but it has a proof, and the proof shows what kind of knowing it is: no act without will, no will without knowledge (U7.12-1 JS P019:8-10; AH P068:2-3, 8-9, one line from B7.E.8, H08). [frame; B7.E.8 is H08's point, one line here]
  2. بنی ہوئی چیز اپنے بنانے والے کے بارے میں کیا کہتی ہے؟ Its order and fineness need a knowing source: the galaxy's parts held by ratios so fine that a millimetre undoes them, billions of branches of science in one work, so its source is a knowledge without end; the world's many sciences need one source beyond matter; a carpet's pattern cannot come from loose threads (U7.12-1 AH P102:2-11 with 67:14 tail; U7.12-2 AH P146:1-6, JS P147:1-16). [PART B7.E.33: recall B3 S19 / B3 S10 / B1 S11 "the order of creation rests on knowledge behind it; the watch and the car show a maker" then new: the conclusion stated as the attribute, AH P102, P146, JS P147:9-13; SAME RR3-001, RR3-002 recalled]
  3. خالق کا عالم ہونا کیوں ضروری ہے؟ He encompasses what He makes: the whole being, make and every aspect of a creature hangs on Him every instant, so He knows it; «أَلَا يَعْلَمُ مَنْ خَلَقَ وَهُوَ اللَّطِيفُ الْخَبِيرُ» (U7.12-1 JS P103:2-7, P124:5-12, 67:13-14 block; U7.12-3 JS P171:2-34 mental garden, P177:2-23 two aspects). [CORE REPEAT B7.E.16, kept in full: recall B3 S10 "the maker must encompass and know what he makes (67:14)" then taught; SAME B7.E.9 and B7.E.17 recalled]
  4. ائمہ نے یہ دلیل کیسے برتی، اور خود اللہ نے اسے کیسے کہا؟ The maker-or-made debate, told once (U7.12-1 JS P122:1-17, P125, P005:1-11 merged with U7.12-3 JS P177:25-31, P178:2-11); and God's own word to the people of Paradise: «كيف لا أعلم وأنا خلقتكم وأسكنت أرواحكم في أبدانكم» (U7.12-1 MM P101:1-9, one sentence of setting). [within the CORE REPEAT B7.E.16 (B3 S10 tells the debate in full): debate under the recall, flagged; new: MM's hadith qudsi, taught]
  5. پھر وہ کتنا قریب ہے؟ Nearer than the jugular vein, because He knows: because Creator, Knower; because Knower, near; He knows the eye's treachery and what breasts hide (50:16 block; U7.12-1 JS P044:2-9, P005:25-28; AH P043:10-11 with 40:19). Hand-off line: the fruit, shame and watchfulness (AH P043:12, EZ P051, JS P050, MM P042:16-17), is H09 (U7.14). [PART B7.E.5: recall B3 S10 / B1 S18 / B1 S15 "Creator so Knower, Knower so Near (50:16); be ashamed before the One who is present" then new: AH's 40:19 line (P043:10-11); the soul-body sample is in the recall (see Repeats); SAME RR4-002, RR2-014 recalled]
  6. کیا مخلوق کی باریکی بھی یہی دکھاتی ہے؟ Yes: the gnat and Nimrod, told short: the smallest soldier of His air army, its sting a thousandth of its body yet hollow like a needle, felled the king who claimed lordship; «وهو اللطيف الخبير» (U7.12-3 AH P182:2-8, 11-21, 26-42; Pharaoh's «أنا ربكم الأعلى» at P182:25 not put in Nimrod's mouth). [NEW B7.E.32; SAME B7.E.34 recalled in one line: the Quran speaks from this encompassing knowledge]

Next lesson picks up

اس کا علم کہاں تک پہنچتا ہے: کیا غیب، راز اور آشکار اس کے لیے یکساں ہیں؟ (S07)

S07 ہر راز اللہ کے نزدیک آشکار اور ہر غیب اس کے سامنے حاضر ہے؛ چھپنا ہماری طرف کا پردہ ہے، اس کی طرف کوئی پردہ نہیں

ledger · 20 new · 5 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. What does He know? The keys of the unseen are His alone; every leaf that falls, every grain in the earth's darkness, the wet and the dry (6:59 block once, MM P001:1-5 frame; JS P004:3-14 in one line and P004:16-20 the hadith line on «ورقة، حبة، أرض»); He gives a key of the unseen to no one absolutely, only in a set measure (AH P017:12-12, P017:18-19). (E.1) [NEW; AH re-read RR2-007 SAME: recall only]
  2. What are «غیب» and «شہادت»? What is beyond our senses (JS P028:3-5, 8); and, by al-Sadiq, «الغيب ما لم يكن، والشهادة ما قد كان» (MM P016:12); the two senses do not conflict (JS P028:6-9); 13:8-9 as the ayah that names Him «عالم الغيب والشهادة» (MM P001:6). (E.4) new
  3. Is the hidden harder for Him than the open? No: «كل سر عندك علانية، وكل غيب عندك شهادة» (MM P056:3 cut to the two clauses; JS P059:6-20 the degrees of hiddenness); hiddenness is relative to us, who have walls and veils, and He has none (JS P057:13-15); the Quran says it again and again: secret and open, the atom's weight (MM P016:4-6, one ayah block, 34:3 inline; JS P029:9-15, P019:1-5, P037:2-7 with 57:4); the hoopoe's «يخرج الخبء... ويعلم ما تخفون وما تعلنون» (EZ P170:3-6, JS P169:8-11; 27:25 block, 27:24 and 27:26 one line of setting); the Prophet's «ولا اشتبهت عليه الخفيات، له الإحاطة بكل شيء» (JS U7.2-1 P012:6-12). (E.6, E.3, E.53, A.32; E.75, E.78 recalled) [E.6 NEW; E.53 NEW; E.3 core repeat kept in full: recall B3 S10 and B1 S18 then the verse set as a knowledge lesson; A.32 PART: recall B2 S10 and B6 S02 then the attribute itself with the Ghadir line and Nahj 109; E.75, E.78 SAME: recall only]
  4. What then is «الباطن»? Inward to things by His knowledge, not by entering them: «هو الظاهر عليها بسلطانه وعظمته، والباطن لها بعلمه ومعرفته» (MM P083:3; MM P083:1-2 block only if Ali keeps it, see Asks); al-Rida: «وأما الباطن فليس على معنى الاستبطان للأشياء... علماً وحفظاً وتدبيراً» (JS P092:4-9); «الحمد لله الذي بطن خفيات الأمور» and its two readings, both His knowing (JS P085:1-10, P090:5-7, P091:2-4, P085:12-27); His manifestness is His hiddenness (AH P084:1-4). (E.10) new
  5. How fine does it reach in creation? The falling leaf, the branch it fell from and the wind or rain that felled it; He knows particulars as particulars, with no change in His Essence (JS P011:3-17, P010:3, P135:1-2, P010:5-15 one Nahj text, Teacher Copy); what is unknown to all and has no outward being yet is known to Him: «عارف بالمجهول، معروف عند كل جاهل» (JS P151:4-9); every scattered particle is with Him «من لا يعزب عنه مثقال ذرة في ظلمات الأرض ويعلم عدد الأشياء ووزنها» (MM P175:8, cut to that clause); fine layer: «وعلمه تحت التحت» (MM P174:2), a reckoning minds cannot reach (MM P179:2, one clause). (E.2, E.42, E.14, E.13, E.18) [E.2, E.14 NEW; E.13, E.18 NEW, Teacher Copy; E.42 PART: recall B2 S08 then new: the first half dwelt on for what does not yet exist, JS P151:5-9]
  6. Does it reach inside us? The glance and the word (JS P135:4-21, «شخوص لحظة... كرور لفظة»); the secret of the concealer, the whisper, the passing thought and the firm resolve: «وخواطر رجم الظنون وعقد عزيمات اليقين» (JS P152:4-22, Nahj 91 block once); the eye's theft (JS P152:24-29; 40:19 inline from AH P043:10-11; JS P154:4-12 Nahj 90 in one line, breaths counted); shown or hidden, He knows: 2:284 (JS P164:3-10; AH re-read RR3-026); passing thoughts are not tried until spoken (EZ P165:5-7, with its Ask); hidden desires and the night's darkness (JS P205:7 or P205:2, one of them, with the two-reading note P205:11-19, 61-65; P205:66-69); He hears every word, even unspoken in the heart, even under Yazid's palace (EZ P166:5-7). (E.43, E.52, E.58, E.76) [E.43 PART: recall B1 S18 "40:19" then new: the Nahj 91 and 90 detail, JS P152, P154; E.52 PART: recall B1 S18 (2:284 with 40:19, read by AH in B3 S07, B4 S19) then new: EZ P165:5-7 and JS P164:4-10; E.58, E.76 NEW; AH re-reads RR2-016, RR2-042 SAME: recall only]
  7. Does anything distract or veil Him? No affair distracts Him from another, because His knowledge and power have no limit (JS P196:3-11, with the Nahj wording, see Asks); no heaven veils a heaven, no earth an earth (JS P191:2 block; P191:10-12; P192:3-16 the dead as the living, the high as the low); near and far are equal before Him: «إنما منظره في القرب والبعد سواء» (JS P066:4 block, P066:2-3, 8-16); His knowledge pierced the veils and knows the secrets (JS P062:5-11 or P064:1-17, one of them; P065:4-5 «ذلك في علمه وإحاطته وقدرته سواء»); fine layer: all lights fade before the light of His knowledge (JS P197, in Urdu only); the ring in the desert (JS P195, Teacher Copy, see Asks). (E.46, E.44, E.7, E.47, E.45) [E.46, E.44, E.7 NEW (E.44 merged with E.7 as the ledger asks); E.47 NEW, Teacher Copy; E.45 PART: recall B3 S05 then new: the darkness and light layers and «as if He had not created», JS P195:13-17, Teacher Copy]
  8. What follows for our deeds? A mustard seed in a rock, the heavens or the earth, God brings it (31:16, MM P097:4, JS P098:5-11; 21:47 inline, JS U7.22-1 P076:2-6); deeds need no scale, because He knows the count and weight of things: «لأن الأعمال ليست أجساماً... وإن الله لا يخفى عليه شيء»; the scale is justice (JS P203:17-35; 99:7-8 printed once, here, P203:30); the servant made to remember every glance, word and step when given his book (MM P134:3-6, al-Sadiq, Ayyashi); fine layer: only God knows the reward of feeding a believer (JS P202:8-11, one line). (E.15, H.70, E.57, E.19, E.56) [E.15, H.70, E.57, E.19 NEW; E.56 NEW, Teacher Copy; AH re-read RR4-012 PART: recall B2 S05 then new: the Day secrets are tested; RR2-040, RR1-032 NONE, Teacher Copy]

Arabic printed again

«إنما منظره في القرب والبعد سواء لم يبعد منه قريب ولم يقرب منه بعيد» (JS P066:4; already printed in full in B5 S10, al-Kazim's answer, same text); «عارف بالمجهول، معروف عند كل جاهل» (JS U7.12-2 P151:4 block; already printed in full in B2 S08); «فأما هو فلا يخفى عليه خافية في آناء الليل والنهار» (JS P006:6, Teacher Copy at step 5; already printed in full in B6 S03, and within al-Rida's matn in B2 S11, B3 S10, B3 S18); «كل شيء خاشع له، وكل شيء قائم به، غنى كل فقير... ومفزع كل ملهوف» (the opening run of MM P056:3; already printed in full in B2 S08 and B6 S02; the claim clauses «كل سر عندك علانية...» are new; if Ali takes the cut to the two clauses, the Handout drops the run and only the Teacher Copy prints it, flagged); 101:6-9 «فأما من ثقلت موازينه...» (JS U7.22-1 P076:8-14, H.70, Teacher Copy; already printed in full in B1 S13); 2:255 «الله لا إله إلا هو الحي القيوم...» (JS P195:18, E.45, Teacher Copy; already printed in full in B5 S08). Inline only here (not counted, earlier use noted): «الحمد لله الذي بطن خفيات الأمور» (full in B3 S04, B5 S03), 3:5 (full in B5 S08), «عظم الخالق في أنفسهم» (full in B1 S18, B2 S09, B3 S07), 64:4 opening (full in B2 S10, B5 S02, B5 S05), 11:6 (inline B4 S03), 34:3 (inline B4 S11), 58:7 (inline B2 S06, B5 S11), 2:284 (inline B1 S06, B1 S18, B3 S07, B4 S19).

Step 6 is otherwise JS and EZ; step 7 is JS alone: AH and MM are absent from step 7 (no affair distracts Him, no heaven veils a heaven, near and far equal), and the ledger note's MM 7.7.5 (Nahj 109 for E.7) is the only MM thread to it. EZ adds in steps 3 (hoopoe), 6 (passing thoughts; hearing in the heart) and the SAME recalls.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «وَعِندَهُ مَفَاتِحُ الْغَيْبِ لَا يَعْلَمُهَا إِلَّا هُوَ ۚ وَيَعْلَمُ مَا فِي الْبَرِّ وَالْبَحْرِ ۚ وَمَا تَسْقُطُ مِن وَرَقَةٍ إِلَّا يَعْلَمُهَا...» (6:59; MM P001:5).
  2. «الغيب ما لم يكن، والشهادة ما قد كان» (Imam al-Sadiq, Ma'ani al-Akhbar; Jami FOUND, Nawadir al-Akhbar 1:93, same wording).
  3. «كل سر عندك علانية، وكل غيب عندك شهادة» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj sermon 109, Subhi al-Salih 1:92; Jami FOUND; MM's block opens «كل شيء خاشع له», Ibn Maytham «خاشع», 'Abduh «خاضع»).
  4. «هو الظاهر عليها بسلطانه وعظمته، وهو الباطن لها بعلمه ومعرفته» (Amir al-Mu'minin; Jami FOUND, Sharh 'Abduh 2; the print drops «وهو»), with al-Rida «وأما الباطن فليس على معنى الاستبطان للأشياء بأن يغور فيها ولكن... علماً وحفظاً وتدبيراً» (al-Kafi 1; Jami FOUND).
  5. «وخواطر رجم الظنون وعقد عزيمات اليقين» (Nahj sermon 91, Subhi al-Salih 1:61-62; Jami FOUND), with 40:19 «يَعْلَمُ خَائِنَةَ الْأَعْيُنِ وَمَا تُخْفِي الصُّدُورُ» inline.
  6. «الحمد لله الذي لا توارى عنه سماء سماء ولا أرض أرضا» (Nahj, Sharh 'Abduh 2; Jami FOUND) and «إنما منظره في القرب والبعد سواء» (al-Kafi 1:175; Jami FOUND).
  7. 31:16 «يَا بُنَيَّ إِنَّهَا إِن تَكُ مِثْقَالَ حَبَّةٍ مِّنْ خَرْدَلٍ...» and al-Sadiq «لا، لأن الأعمال ليست أجساماً وإنما هي صفة ما عملوا... إن الله لا يخفى عليه شيء» (al-Ihtijaj; Tafsir al-Safi 2:181, Nawadir al-Akhbar 1:342; Jami FOUND).

اگر اللہ ہر راز اور ہر غیب کو جانتا ہے تو کیا وہ چیزوں کو ان کے ہونے کے بعد جانتا ہے یا ہونے سے پہلے بھی، اور اس کے علم کے سامنے ہمارا علم کیا ہے؟ (S08)

S08 اللہ ہر چیز کو اس کے ہونے سے پہلے جانتا ہے؛ اس کا علم نہ سیکھا ہوا ہے نہ کہیں ختم ہوتا ہے، اور ہمارا سارا علم اسی کی عطا کا ایک قطرہ ہے

ledger · 18 new · 7 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. Does He know a thing only once it is? No: He knows what will be before it is, and His knowing before is as His knowing after; the Ka'ba foretold and fulfilled shows a knowledge not bound by time and place. (E.29: MM P140:1, JS P159:1-2, JS P142:8-10, EZ P143:3-6; E.41: JS P190:1-18, 5:97; E.31 one simple sentence, TC: MM P145:1, «التروك»; RR1-036 recall; pointer to S10 for qada and qadar, E.54) [NEW for E.29, E.41, E.31 | SAME: recall only for RR1-036, "Recall B1 S06: God does not test us to find out; He is Knower of the secret"]
  2. Where does the Quran show it? The angels foretold bloodshed and were answered «إِنِّي أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ»; al-Rida reads the verse as His knowledge before things. (E.55: 2:30, 2:33; JS P200:1-14, al-Rida's report cut to the claim; JS P200:8 «فزت ورب الكعبة» line TC only; the Hujjas' names line only if sourced) new
  3. Is His knowledge learned, increased or ending? He knows without acquiring, without increase and without a knowledge taken from another, since acquiring would mean need; «لَا تَقُولَنَّ مُنْتَهى عِلْمِهِ»; encompassing belongs to His knowledge. (E.48: JS P157:4-32, JS P156:4-12; E.49: JS P198:2-7, one of the two JS texts; E.51: JS P159:6-9, Kafi 1:159, MM 7.8.26 same report; JS P162:1-22 reconciliation in Urdu only; H.29: JS P192:1-9) ↺ B1 S12Already taught: B1 S12 "divine knowledge differs in essence from taught, acquired knowledge"New here: the attribute itself, the Knower without acquisition (Nahj, JS P157:13-32; EZ «العالم غير المعلم» P158:7-8) and JS's rule that acquisition means need (P156:4-10) | NEW for E.49, E.51 | PART: recall B5 S11 "the Throne as knowledge and power; istiwa' as encompassing power" then new: JS's split, encompassing to knowledge and dominance to power (P192:1-9)
  4. What is ours beside His? Minds do not reach Him while His knowledge encompasses them; the knowers' knowledge falls short before His: a bird's beak of the sea, a needle tip, a zero beside a number; the messengers say «لا علم لنا سواك», and the Prophet «I know only what my Lord taught me». (E.8: MM P067:1-3 cut to «أحاط بها علمه... ولا ما في السماوات العلى والأرضين السفلى», AH P068:2-3 one line; E.11: MM P094:1-4, MM P096:1-2, AH P095:1-2, 17:85; E.27: MM P137:7, AH P138:5-7, 20:114; TC fine layer: A.27, A.45, A.48, E.12, E.37) [NEW for E.8 | PART: recall B1 S11 "MM brings the bird's beak line for the Imams holding the prophets' knowledge" and B1 S07 "the Prophet's knowledge is tiny beside God's" then new: the claim as a lesson on His knowledge, the Ikhtisas report, AH's needle tip and zero (P095:1-2), 17:85 | PART: recall B1 S07 as above then new: 5:109 with al-Sadiq (MM P137:7), AH's «لا أعلم إلا ما علمني ربي» with 20:114 (P138:5-7)]
  5. Are there things He keeps to Himself? The Hour, and the time of the reappearance like it; what the wombs truly hold, not only boy or girl. (E.35: EZ P143:19-20, AH P148:13-19 subject to the ASK, EZ P210:3-6 and P210:33-36 read as the reappearance, MM al-Baqir report (MM 7.29.11); RR4-038 TC; E.80: EZ P209:1-43, P210:7-11, P210:19-27, 31:34; E.83 TC: EZ P215:9-12, «He knows he will not repent» only) new
  6. Does He tell His Messenger what He wills? Yes: «نَبَّأَنِيَ الْعَلِيمُ الْخَبِيرُ» (66:3), and "this knowledge is His, unless He Himself tells" (EZ P209:42-43); the key is given in measure (recall S07, AH P017:19, one line). (E.82: EZ P213:14-25, P214:9-16; background story ASK) new
  7. Close: what does this ask of us? Not to judge or curse on our own knowledge, since what has passed in His knowledge we do not know: Salman did not curse his torturers until the Prophet told him. (E.85: EZ P217:14-20, P218:11-13, P217:29-31; TC fine layer: E.84, E.86, E.87, E.89; the dua texts go to S27) ↺ B4 S19Already taught: B4 S19 "EZ tells Salman refusing to curse his torturers lest a sayer of la ilaha illa Allah among their descendants be lost"New here: the same story read for God's prior knowledge and the Prophet's confirmation, in EZ P217:14, P218:11-13, P217:29-31

the Ikhtisas report «قَالَ إِنَّمَا سَأَلْتُکَ عَنِ الْمَکَانِ... فَقَصُرَ عِلْمُ الْعُلَمَاءِ عِنْدَ عِلْمِهِ» (MM P094:1-4; already printed in full in B5 S11, checked word for word); «الْمُقَدِّرِ لِجَمِیعِ الْأُمُورِ بِلَا رَوِیَّةٍ وَ لَا ضَمِیرٍ» (JS P198:8; already printed in full in B5 S02, checked), if it stays (see Ask 14). Rows checked and not listed: «قدرته بان بها» (MM P067) shares only its opening clause with B4 S12, whose block stops at «وبانت الأشياء منه», and the claim clause «أحاط بها علمه» is not there, so printed normally; «إن الله يعلم ما في الأرحام» (EZ P209) is a short run of 31:34 inside B3 S13's Nahj 83 text, not the same text; the Prophet's light report (EZ P219, B2 S19) shares only its long Jami matn, and the three short excerpts printed here were not found in B2 S19. Who carries it

MM and AH carry steps 1 (MM P140:1), 4 (MM P067, P094, P096, P137; AH P095, P138) and 5 in part (AH P148, under the ASK; MM's al-Baqir report). AH is absent from step 1 once RR1-036 is recalled, and absent from steps 2, 3, 6 and 7. This is the most JS/EZ-heavy of the three U7.12 lessons, as the diagnosis said.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «كان الله عز وجل ولا شيء غيره ولم يزل عالما بما يكون فعلمه به قبل كونه كعلمه به بعد كونه» (JS P159:1; Jami: al-Baqir via Muhammad b. Muslim, al-Kafi 1:159, as printed; the lesson names no Imam) with MM «العالم بما يكون قبل أن يكون» (P140:1).
  2. 2:30 «إِنِّي أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ», 2:33, with al-Rida's report (JS P200:1; Jami OTHER-WORDING: Mustadrak Safinat al-Bihar 7:330-331, «فقد علم الله... لما نهوا عنه»: print «فقط» is a slip and the line is cut at «عن»); 5:97 (JS P190).
  3. «لَا تَقُولَنَّ مُنْتَهى عِلْمِهِ؛ فَلَيْسَ لِعِلْمِهِ مُنْتَهىً، وَلكِنْ قُلْ: مُنْتَهى رِضَاهُ» (JS P159:8; Jami: al-Kafi 1:159-160, as printed); Nahj «الْعَالِمِ بِلَا اكْتِسَابٍ وَ لَا ازْدِیَادٍ وَ لَا عِلْمٍ مُسْتَفَادٍ» (in the Jami matn of JS P198:8, Bihar 4:319-320; proposed in place of «المقدر», Ask 14); Nahj 83 «لَهُ الْإِحَاطَةُ بِكُلِّ شَیْءٍ» (JS P192; Jami FOUND-OTHER-SPEAKER: Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj 83, with «القوة» where JS has «القدرة»; full sermon line printed in S07, recall here).
  4. «ما أَخَذْتَ مِنْ عِلْمِ رَبِّكَ مَا حَمَلَ ظَهْرُ مِنْقَارِی مِنْ جَمِیعِ الْبَحْرِ» (MM P096:1-2; Jami: al-'Ayyashi via Kanz al-Daqa'iq 8:119, al-Burhan; the print drops the opening «ما»); Ikhtisas «فَقَصُرَ عِلْمُ الْعُلَمَاءِ عِنْدَ عِلْمِهِ» (MM P094; al-Ikhtisas 1:60-61, as printed; reprint flagged B5 S11); Tawhid «لكنه سبحانه أحاط بها علمه» (MM P067; Jami FOUND, small variants «تعبير اللغات»).

  6. 31:34 «إِنَّ اللَّهَ عِنْدَهُ عِلْمُ السَّاعَةِ... وَيَعْلَمُ مَا فِي الْأَرْحَامِ» (EZ P209, P210) with al-Sadiq to al-Mufaddal «وَ هُوَ السَّاعَةُ الَّتِی قَالَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَی یَسْئَلُونَكَ عَنِ السَّاعَةِ» (EZ P210:4; Jami: Bihar 53:3, Mukhtasar al-Basa'ir, as printed; «هو» is the Qa'im's rising); EZ «إنما مثله كمثل الساعة» (P143:19-20).

Next lesson picks up

If He knows every secret and sees every act, how is one to live under His sight? (S09)

S09 اللہ ہمیں وہاں بھی دیکھتا ہے جہاں کوئی نہیں دیکھتا، اور یہی جاننا بندے کو گناہ سے روکتا اور اس سے جوڑتا ہے

ledger · 7 new · 8 part-taught before · 10 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Does God see what we do even when no one else does? Yes: secrets, hearts, glances, solitude; He hears and sees His servants; nothing in us is outside His sight. MM H1 P011:6-7 (text 1, the clause «ولا تهتكوا أستاركم عند من يعلم أسراركم» only); AH H1 P012:1 (89:14, 41:40 inline); EZ 20:46 «إنني معكما أسمع وأرى» and AH 41:40 (E.77); EZ H2 P067:9-41 «يلحظ كل عين», a glance of care, not yara (E.94); one dua line, JS H1 P039:14 «سبحان من هو مطلع على خوازن القلوب» (text 8, E.68); fine layer: 41:53 in one line (JS P061, E.70). (E.21 core, E.77, E.94, E.68, E.70). [E.21: core repeat kept in full, see Repeats | E.77 NEW | E.94 PART: recall B6 S02 "the Ghadir line, He observes every eye and eyes do not see Him" then new: EZ's word note, yalhazu is an attentive glance of care (EZ H2 P067:9-41) | E.68 PART: recall B1 S18 "He knows the hidden, the treachery of eyes (40:19), as ground of watchfulness" then new: Imam Hasan's tasbih (text 8) and Fatima's ta'qib (text 11, TC) | E.70 NEW]
  2. We do not see Him, so how do we live with it? As if seeing Him, since He sees us; to think He does not see is kufr; to hide from people and not from Him is to make Him the least of watchers; the Abu Basir story shows it in solitude. JS H1 P017:8 (text 6, al-Sadiq to Ishaq b. Ammar, extended to «فقد جعلته من أهون الناظرين إليك»), P018:3-5; JS P019:3 (90:7 one line); EZ H1 P016 (Abu Basir, «لا تعد», after the Jami fix); AH H1 P012:1 (the child, inside E.21); EZ H1 P021:20-23 for the policeman only. (E.21) [E.21: core repeat kept in full; the claim, AH's child and 'if you do not see Him He sees you' are recalled from B1 S18 and B6 S07 and the new texts are taught: Ali's 'do not tear your covers', the Ishaq hadith with its continuation, Abu Basir, 90:7]
  3. Why has He set witnesses over us if He already knows? So that the servant, knowing they are with him, holds back; the recording angels write every word. MM H1 P025:1-4 (82:10 inline, text 16 Nahj 85, text 18 al-Sadiq to the zindiq); AH re-read ZH-S31 ¶26-27 (RR1-040, two watchers record every word); EZ H1 P010:5-7 (He needs no witnesses; the proof is completed on others); JS P029 (50:17-18) one line only. (E.23, E.20 part, RR1-040). [E.23 PART: recall B1 S18 "AH's youth at Imam Rida's shrine: 'God is present and watching'; watchfulness with 40:19" then new: the recording angels as witnesses, MM P025:1-4 and text 18, JS P029:3-11 on 50:16-18 | RR1-040 NEW]
  4. Who else will testify, and against whom? The limbs: they thought God did not know; hands and feet speak by His power; not against the believer, and the places of prayer testify for him. AH H1 P002:1-3 (36:65); AH re-read ZH-S31 ¶22-55 (RR1-041, the sinner who blames the scribes until his hands and feet testify); MM H1 P001:2 (41:22 only), P001:3 (text 22), P001:4-5 (text 23); EZ P010:9-16 (41:21 one line); EZ U7.22 P082 line "He who gave the tongue speech can give it to the hand" fine layer (H.91). (E.20, H.91, RR1-041). [E.20 PART: recall B1 S07 "the limbs testify on the Day (24:24-25), there for knowing God as the manifest Truth" then new: as His knowledge and power at the reckoning, 41:22, 36:65, God needs no witnesses (AH P002, MM P001:2-5, EZ P010:5-7) | H.91 NEW | RR1-041 NEW]
  5. What, then, is "fear of the station of the Lord"? Knowing that He sees, hears and knows, when that knowing holds one back from the ugly act. JS H2 P053:11-26 (text 33, al-Kafi, the key text), 55:46 and 79:40-41, P056:16-23. (E.67). [E.67 PART: recall B1 S18 "fear of the station of one's Lord (55:46, 79:40) is the measure of knowing Him" then new: al-Sadiq's gloss, the one who fears is he who knows God sees and hears him and it bars him from the ugly (JS P053:11-26)]
  6. Can one hide behind a lawful form, or deceive people and so deceive Him? No: if presentation before God is true, why the trick? EZ H2 P068:6-14 (text 45), P069:12-14, P069:23-32 (stratagems that keep the outward lawful). (E.95, E.96). [E.95 PART: recall B1 S18 "the knower does not cheat a naive customer because God is present" then new: al-Sadiq's 'if presentation before God is true, why deceit?' (EZ P068:6-14) | E.96 NEW]
  7. What does His seeing give the one who is wronged? Comfort: it is before God's eye. AH H1 P030:1-4 (text 27, al-Husayn at his infant's death, made a block); JS P031 (Fatima's 'under God's eye is what you do') one line; EZ H1 P038:4-17 (52:48 printed alone). (E.39). new
  8. And what bond grows from living before Him? He is with every secret talk and listens; He guards whom He loves; prayer is standing before the One who listens. JS H1 P042:14-18 (text 12, «يا صاحب كل نجوى»); MM 58:7 one line (recalled from its first print in this Bab, U7.12); JS H2 P063 (He guards whom He loves, fine layer); JS H2 P064-P065 (prayer, 'My servant, I am your listener', no text, see Asks); TC: the Mi'raj hadith and Sahifa 5 «ولا تفضحنا عندك» (JS P058-P059, no text found). (E.73, E.71, E.72, E.69). [E.73 NEW | E.71 NEW | E.72 PART: recall B1 S18 "attending in prayer to the God before whom one stands gives humility" then new: 'My servant, I am your listener' and its effect on asking (JS P064, P065; no text) | E.69 PART (TC): recall B1 S18 "living under His watching gaze, muraqaba" then new: the Mi'raj hadith 'he keeps My knowledge and gaze upon him' and 'do not disgrace us before You' (JS P058, P059)]

    • B7.E.95: base "Recall B1 S18: the knower does not cheat a naive customer because God is present"; new: al-Sadiq's 'if presentation before God is true, why deceit?', EZ H2 P068:6-14 (step 6).
  • Core repeat kept in full: B7.E.21 (live mindful before the One who knows the secret: He sees you though you think none sees). Taught in B1 S18 (the knower refrains in private, the least of watchers, AH's watching child, 40:19, 'if you do not see Him, He sees you') and B6 S07 (the Abu Dharr line printed in full). It is this lesson's own core claim (steps 1 and 2), so it is kept in full with the recall named. Doubt read (E.21 SAME or PART): the claim and AH's example are taught; the narrations are new (MM's 'do not tear your covers' is in no Bab 1 to 6 lesson; Abu Basir and 90:7 are not in B1 S18; the Ishaq continuation «فقد كفرت ... أهون الناظرين» is only an Ask in B6 S07). Verdict left SAME, handled by rule 3 as the ledger already says. core repeat · ask keep E.21 in full here with AH's child and the 'He sees you' line, or keep it in full in B1 S18 / B6 S07 and teach here only the new texts (text 1, the Ishaq hadith with its continuation, Abu Basir, 90:7) after a one-line recall?
  • Rule 7: none.

Next lesson picks up

اگر اللہ پہلے سے جانتا تھا کہ میں کیا کروں گا، تو کیا میں وہ کرنے پر مجبور نہیں؟ (If God knew beforehand what I would do, am I not forced to do it?) (S10) Note on the counts: SAME recalled = 0 points (the one SAME point, E.21, is the core repeat) + 10 SAME RR records; RR 12 = 10 SAME + 2 NONE (RR1-040, RR1-041, taught); Arabic reprinted 2 = 79:40-41 and 41:53, plus 2 more if kept (the Abu Dharr line, the rahim hadith).

S10 اللہ کا پہلے سے جاننا بندے کا اختیار نہیں چھینتا: علم جو ہونے والا ہے اسے ظاہر کرتا ہے، اسے کرواتا نہیں

ledger · 15 new · 2 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. If God knew beforehand what I would do, am I not forced to do it? The doubt starts from the prior news of Imam Husayn's martyrdom (the chapter heading «اخبار الله تعالى به شهادته» told in Urdu) and is put in the verse attributed to Khayyam, «منسوب», no other names (EZ P090:1-4, P033:1-8, P067:2-6; one line of JS P064:6-14; B7.F.59, B7.F.42). Lead-in, if Ali agrees: the questioner's words to al-Sadiq «من أين لحق الشقاء أهل المعصية حتى حكم الله لهم في علمه بالعذاب على عملهم؟» (MM U7.12-3 P181:3; B7.E.30). The wall verse (EZ P090:4) is poetry, not a Masum's word: Urdu, marked as a verse, or TC. new
  2. Does knowing a thing make it happen? No: knowledge discloses, it does not cause; His knowledge matches what will really be, «علم واقع کے مطابق ہے» (JS P022:13-22; one example only: the physician JS P012:3-11, or EZ's unused sunrise forecast P004; B7.F.43, B7.F.48). [NEW; see Ask 24 on a B2 S18 base]
  3. But His knowledge is certain and ours is not: does certainty change this? No: the wine-makers in the verse were certain too (EZ P067:15-23); His knowledge is unlike ours in every way but this one (JS P018:1-10); the mirror in one line, and knowing is not approving (EZ P072:7-16, JS P069, JS P071 with 6:28 inline; B7.F.44). No ikhtilaf box: JS P079 reconciles «بلا تعین» (Ask 4). new
  4. Do the Masumin say it? Yes: Amir al-Mu'minin at Siffin, «...و كان كعلمه فيه»: the Prophet was, by his own choice, as God knew him (EZ P089:1-15, texts 28, 29; B7.F.58); and Amir al-Mu'minin on qada and qadar: not «وكلهم الله إلى أنفسهم», not «جبرهم على المعاصي», but «الخير بتوفيق الله والشر بخذلان الله وكل سابق في علم الله» (JS U7.12-4 P199:2-5; B7.E.54). new
  5. Then what do "the wretched is wretched in his mother's womb" and the clay narrations mean? The wretched is the one God knew, in the womb, would do the deeds of the wretched (JS P051:7-11, texts 16, 17); He gave each a clay suited to the choice He knew, and «ولا ألزم أحدا إلا ما عرفته منه قبل أن أخلقه» with «أنا عدل لا أجور» (JS P042:1-10, P037:4, P037:39-41, texts 7, 10; B7.F.47). The fuller statement is al-Sadiq's answer: «فلما حكم بذلك وهب لأهل محبته القوة على معرفته... لسبق علمه فيهم... فوافقوا ما سبق لهم في علمه», with 8:23 (MM U7.12-3 P181:4-17; B7.E.30). ↺ B2 S14 · B2 S04Already taught: B2 S14 "no compulsion in the dharr; the clays followed their own choice; 'the wretched in his mother's womb' read by choice" (also B2 S04: the clay's impurity has an effect but does not compel)New here: the clay suited to the choice He knew, read through His knowledge (JS P042:1-10, P037:39-41, P051:7-11) and MM's al-Sadiq hadith. RR1-037: SAME, recall only
  6. And "God has sealed their hearts" (2:7)? A punishment for the unbelief they chose: when He knew they would not turn back, He withheld His help and grace; al-Rida: «الختم هو الطبع على قلوب الكفار عقوبة على كفرهم», with the clause of 4:155 «بل طبع الله عليها بكفرهم» (JS P086:1-17, texts 19 to 22; B7.F.50). ↺ B1 S08Already taught: B1 S08 "He made their hearts deviate (61:5) after they deviated, so they stay answerable"New here: the seal of 2:7 as punishment, al-Rida's word, JS P086:1-17
  7. Why, then, were the prophets, the Imams and Fatima favoured above others? He chose His friends knowing their outcome; His grace follows His knowledge of who would choose servanthood most; Dua al-Nudba: «بعد أن شرطت عليهم الزهد في درجات هذه الدنيا... و علمت منهم الوفاء به»; nothing but good has issued from Him: He made man free, and it is enough to know there is no wrong in His act (JS P054:12-21, P058:4-10, EZ P059:6 one line, JS P087:14-24, texts 26, 31, 32; JS U7.41-3 P132:1-11; B7.F.49, B7.F.51, B7.O.30). new
  8. Did the prior news of Karbala take the killers' choice from them? No: the news reported their choice; the proof was completed on them to the last moment (EZ P082:1-9, P083:2-18, the Hurr and Umar Sa'd line; JS P081 Abu Lahab is the same point, one line; B7.F.45). Landing: knowledge discloses, it does not compel; man's freedom and God's free agency both stand (JS P085:17-20), which opens power (S11) and the free Doer (U7.24). new

Who carries it

JS and EZ. JS carries the answer and the narrations (steps 2 to 7), EZ the doubt, Siffin and Karbala (steps 1, 3, 4, 8). The Fasl itself has no AH or MM sentence. MM comes in only through B7.E.30 (his al-Sadiq hadith moved from U7.12-3, step 5, and the lead-in of step 1) and the four kept imports (TC only, Claude's framing). AH is absent: his one record (RR1-037) is SAME and only recalled.

AH re-read adds

RR1-037 (U7.18, ZH-S31 ¶14-15): He knew all our inner states and deeds before creating us, so His test is not to learn; SAME (B5 S02 TC, B6 S07; B1 S06), recalled only, TC.

Imports 28
  • Kept B7.G.11 (MM, JS: His knowledge has no causality for the order; realisation is by His will), at step 2. Claude's judgement MM says of God's own knowledge what the lesson says of His knowledge of our acts, that knowing brings nothing about and what happens happens by will, so it supports "knowing does not make it happen" from the side of His act. AH's divergent line (God's knowledge active, the Essence's love the origin of creation, AH 7.13.6) is NOT brought in. Verdict kept NONE (the import doubt says PART if AH's love lines count; they are not this point).
  • Kept B7.G.12 (MM, JS: knowledge discloses both contradictories, so no order is fixed from the side of His knowledge; determination is by His ra'y), at step 2; PART: base "Recall B1 S05: MM: the light of knowledge unveils both contradictories together, since contradiction belongs to engendering, not unveiling"; new: applied to His knowledge, no order is fixed by it. Claude's judgement if knowledge as such only unveils and fixes nothing, His foreknowledge of a free act fixes nothing either; this is the lesson's step 2 in MM's own terms.
  • Kept B7.I.28 (MM, JS: knowledge and will differ; negating the known does not negate knowledge; He knows what He never wills, 17:86), at step 2 or 3. Claude's judgement God knows things He never brings about, so knowing a thing is not making it happen; this supports the step, though MM's own context is will, not the servant's choice.
  • Kept B7.I.54 (MM, JS, EZ: knowing from eternity does not tie His act; MM: that view is a root of compulsion and of "He has finished the matter", MM 7.25.23), at step 8 (landing). Claude's judgement MM names the view that eternal knowledge ties the act as a root of compulsion (jabr); the lesson answers that same view for the servant's act, and EZ's side of this point is the Khayyam verse of step 1 (EZ 7.28.2, 7.28.4), so the framing is close, not borrowed.
  • Dropped B7.K.5: no sound framing: AH's side (knowledge active, love the origin of creation) contradicts step 2; the MM and JS side is B7.G.11, taken above; a contradiction belongs to M3.
  • Dropped B7.G.10: no sound framing: Sadra's order and measure before decree is about the order of His act, not the servant's choice.
  • Dropped B7.G.13: no sound framing: His self-knowledge versus knowledge of the system is a philosophers' question with no bearing on foreknowledge and choice.
  • Dropped B7.G.14: no sound framing for this step: God as agent by will and choice is the free Doer of U7.24 (H14); bringing it here only adds a voice (also PART with B3 S01, B3 S17).
  • Dropped B7.G.15: no sound framing beyond B7.I.54: its force is the eternal world, emptied law and bada' (an M3 debate on God being bound); I.54 already carries MM's "root of compulsion" here.
  • Dropped B7.G.16: no sound framing: the Abbasids and the philosophers is history and polemic, not a step of this lesson (also PART with B1 S12).
  • Dropped B7.G.20: no sound framing: the philosophers' reduction of His will and power is about God's will (U7.30, U7.24), not the servant's choice.
  • Dropped B7.G.23: no sound framing: necessary issuing (fire, the sun) is about God's agency, H14's ground (also PART with B3 S01).
  • Dropped B7.G.24: no sound framing: JS's conditional acceptance of "complete cause" is a technical M3 nuance; no AH or MM text in the row.
  • Dropped B7.G.26: no sound framing: true choice as dominion over doing and leaving is His perfection of choice (H11, H14), not the servant's freedom under His knowledge.
  • Dropped B7.G.29: no sound framing: no preponderant needed between equal options is about His choice (H14); weak overlap (PART with B3 S15).
  • Dropped B7.G.30: no sound framing: the philosophers' eight kinds of agent; no AH or MM text in the row.
  • Dropped B7.G.31: no sound framing: "any act changes God" is a different opponent view (U7.21, U7.24).
  • Dropped B7.G.34: no sound framing: "issuing" is not a Quranic term is about creation, not choice; no AH or MM text (PART with B4 S12).
  • Dropped B7.G.36: no sound framing: the rule of the One concerns natural causes and His power (U7.21); no AH or MM text (PART with B3 S15).
  • Dropped B7.G.7: no sound framing: the philosophers' essential eternal will is the will debate of U7.30 (H15).
  • Dropped B7.G.8: no sound framing: continuous necessary emanation and an eternal world is cosmology, not foreknowledge and choice.
  • Dropped B7.G.9: no sound framing: Sadra on 'inaya is editors' notes in MM's text, not MM's own words.
  • Dropped B7.K.14: no sound framing: "a thing does not exist until necessitated" is about His act, an M3 debate (PART with B3 S01).
  • Dropped B7.I.29: no sound framing here: deniers of bada' and 5:64 belong with bada' (U7.34), which this lesson already sends out (JS P023).
  • Dropped B7.I.30: no sound framing here: "the pen has dried" and al-Rida against Sulayman are bada' material (U7.34); it would reopen what step 3 closes.
  • Dropped B7.I.50: no sound framing: whether He knows the determination before the will is the «بلا تعین» matter sent to U7.35; only JS's text in the row.
