Next lesson picks up

«اس کی رحمت اس کے غضب سے آگے کیوں ہے، اور وہ کتنی وسیع ہے؟» (Why does His mercy come before His wrath, and how wide is it?) (S23)

8. Mercy, pardon, promise and threat S23 to S26

S23 خدا نے اپنا تعارف رحمت سے کرایا ہے اور اس کی رحمت اس کے غضب سے آگے ہے: وہ ماں سے زیادہ مہربان ہے، مومن و کافر سب تک پہنچتی ہے، اس کی ہر عطا تفضل ہے، دینے سے اس کے ہاں کمی نہیں آتی اور روکنا بھی بخل نہیں؛ پس جو اس کے در پر آئے، خالی نہیں لوٹتا

ledger H23 · 43 new · 3 part-taught before · 1 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. How does God introduce Himself? First by mercy: He has written mercy upon Himself; the Quran opens with «الرحمن الرحيم», four times in al-Hamd, so do not teach a God known by wrath; and whoever names Him Rahman and Rahim, He promises a full share of His mercy (JS P144:5 6:12 «كَتَبَ عَلَىٰ نَفْسِهِ الرَّحْمَةَ», key ayah; EZ P105:10-32, once for the lesson, the name at P105:26 dropped (Ask); JS P104:1-12, P104:22 block «أُشْهِدُكُمْ لَأُوَفِّرَنَّ مِنْ رَحْمَتِي حَظَّهُ»; EZ P193:10 2:207's last clause «وَاللَّهُ رَءُوفٌ بِالْعِبَادِ» one line; O.39, O.36). Teacher Copy under this step: O.113 (EZ P188, 55:1-4 the Merciful taught the Quran, one line at most) and O.116 (EZ P192, Bara'a enters without the basmala, by justice). [NEW (O.36, O.39, O.113, O.116)]
  2. Does His mercy weigh more than His wrath? Yes, it outran it; this is the word Gabriel reports at the station no angel or prophet reached: «سبوح قدوس، أنا رب الملائكة والروح، سبقت رحمتي غضبي»; we call Him «يا من وسعت رحمته غضبه»; and the Quran names forgiveness before punishment (MM P001:1-14, one block in Jami wording; AH P004:1; EZ P191:8-12 15:49-50 «نَبِّئْ عِبَادِي أَنِّي أَنَا الْغَفُورُ الرَّحِيمُ» with P191:11-12; O.1, O.115). Re-read RR4-029 (AH SE-S17 ¶61-63, even His taking is grace, «يا من سبقت رحمته غضبه») recalled, not taught. [NEW (O.1, O.115); RR4-029 SAME: recall B3 S04 "AH: in His majesty is beauty; even His taking is grace, for He takes to give better; «يا من سبقت رحمته غضبه»"]
  3. How great is it? All the mercy on earth, of every mother and every creature, is one part of a hundred; ninety-nine He keeps for the Day; and the Prophet swore by the pigeon mother and her chick that God is kinder to His servants than she is to her young (MM P007:6-9, cut at «تسع و تسعين رحمة», the shafa'a tail out (Ask); AH P037:1-75, key text, the proverb at P037:5 in Urdu only; JS P008:15-21 the father who melts at his child's call, one line; EZ P186 only for the oath it adds, P187:8; O.2, O.18). Teacher Copy: O.72 (JS U7.42-2 P077, "raise your head": the Imam's kindness and God's greater, text not found). Within the Bab: S02 B3 gave a mother's love as one sample (AH P028); the full treatment is here. [NEW (O.2, O.18, O.72)]
  4. Whom does it reach? Everyone: His general mercy (rahmaniyya) reaches believer and unbeliever, Moses and Pharaoh, «برحمتك التي وسعت كل شيء»; He keeps His hand of care even over those who turn their backs, giving them being, life and bread; to Moses' "why do You feed Pharaoh?" He answers "shall I not act by the duty of My lordship?"; and His special mercy (rahimiyya) is for those who turn to Him; Rahman is the general, Rahim the special (AH P010:1-12, Kumayl line one block; JS P011:7-16; MM P009:1-3; AH P015:2-3; AH P019:1-3; O.3, O.4, O.5). JS P020 (Pharaoh's 400 years of hilm) goes to S26 as JS himself defers it (P020:18-19). Re-read RR2-002 (BJ-S15 ¶41, 57:28 two portions) recalled; RR2-003, RR5-013, RR5-025 (the two portions as Imam Hasan and Imam Husayn) Teacher Copy, base recalled. Teacher Copy samples under this step (diag Q21, one line each at most): O.52 (charity falls in His hand first, JS P182, P183), O.56 (the believer's child, JS P184:9 «إن الله أحكم وأكرم أن يسلب المؤمن ثمرة فؤاده ثم يعذبه»), O.117 (EZ P193, God is ra'uf to His servants), O.121 (EZ P197, «وكلأك برعايته»), O.122 (EZ P198 to P199, «يرحم الله» in the Imams' prayers). [NEW (O.3, O.4, O.5, O.52, O.56, O.117, O.121, O.122); RR2-002 SAME: recall B1 S08 "AH on 57:28: two portions of mercy, rahmaniyya and rahimiyya, and a light that guides"; RR2-003, RR5-013, RR5-025 PART: recall B1 S08, S11 "the two portions are Hasan and Husayn" then new: each a mercy in his own way, Hasan's peace (truce, sitting) and Husayn's rising both mercy (BJ-S15 ¶56-57, ¶98; MJ-S08 ¶115-117, ¶165; ZH-S13 ¶131), Teacher Copy]
  5. Is what we receive our due? No: He gave before we existed or asked; He gives the one who asks and the one who does not ask; Paradise is by His mercy, not the price of deeds, «ادخلوا الجنة برحمتي», «لن أجزيكم اليوم بأعمالكم ولكن برحمتي»; He does not deal by strict justice but by mercy; an evil intention is not written and a good one is; and on the Day He outgives the most generous servant, «نحن أولى بالجود منك والكرم», and turns to the poor believers as one apologising, «لم أفقركم لهوان بكم علي» (AH P022:1-8 with the Rajab dua «يا من يعطي من سأله يا من يعطي من لم يسأله» in Jami wording, once here as AH carries it, JS P167 to P169 confirm, the asking part to S27/S28; MM P021:5-7, P023:1-9, one of them as a block; EZ P067:3-6 19:2 one line; JS U7.42-1 P021 (O.75); MM U7.41-3 P119:1-8 cut to «برحمتك يا رب» and «ونحن أولى بالجود منك والكرم»; MM P029:2-3 cut to «لم أفقركم لهوان بكم علي»; O.47, O.6, O.123, O.75, O.9, O.8). Within the Bab: «يا مبتدئا بالنعم قبل استحقاقها» is printed in full in S14 (K.15) and recalled here in one line. Re-read RR3-015 (EJ-S12 ¶109-124, the free clinic: the reward of reviving all mankind to all who helped heal one patient) as one line of His reward beyond desert (Ask). Teacher Copy under this step: B.20 (AH U7.3-3 P092:2-8, P064:27-31: jud is giving what is fitting for no return or purpose, so God alone is Jawad; the philosophers' Arabic definition in Urdu only), O.7 (MM P118:2 cut to «فقد سبق رضائي عنهم»), O.76 (JS U7.42-1 P021, mercy upon mercy, taught once with O.75), O.82 (JS U7.42-3 P085, one verse in Ramadan), O.78 (recall only), RR3-023 and RR3-012 (recall only). [NEW (O.47, O.6, O.123, O.75, O.9, O.8, B.20, O.7, O.76, O.82; RR3-015); O.78 SAME: recall B1 S18 "the Mi'raj word «ما عرفني عبد وخشع لي إلا وخشعت له»: a wonder of His generosity"; RR3-023 SAME: recall B1 S16 "AH: we did not exist and asked nothing, and He gave reason and life unasked: «يا مبتدئا بالنعم قبل استحقاقها»"; RR3-012 SAME: recall B1 S16 "AH: in the pure drink of al-Insan the cupbearer is their Lord Himself"]
  6. Does giving lessen Him, and is His withholding miserly? Neither. His treasuries of mercy do not run out; withholding does not enrich Him nor giving impoverish Him («الحمد لله الذي لا يفره المنع والجمود ولا يكديه الإعطاء والجود»), and He is not more generous in what He is asked than in what He is not; every withholder is blamed but He, for His withholding is by wisdom, never by miserliness; He is the Jawad whether He gives or withholds; He is shakur, more appreciative than any; when the servant says "You blessed me and did not find me grateful", the answer is that from the Generous comes only generosity; and more or less of His bounty is His free choice, tied to our thanks and ingratitude (JS U7.21-4 P197:3 «ويا من لا تفنى خزائن رحمته» one line; JS P054:5-21, P056:4-9; JS P051:6-10; JS P153:9-30 (Imam Rida, see Asks); JS P151:4-20 one block in Jami wording; JS P156:3-10 Nahj 91, simple layer only; JS P113, EZ P114 (35:34 «ربنا»); EZ U7.24-2 P145:1-14; H.35, O.23, O.21, B.22, O.44, O.43, O.45, O.42, K.37). AH here only as a claim (B.22, AH U7.3-1 P063:2-3, P063:9-11: He is not miserly but fayyad), the hayula and simple water frame not given (Q7 of U7.3). Teacher Copy under this step: B.21 (AH U7.3-3 P093, on the side of bestowing He is unlimited), K.35 (EZ U7.24-2 P142:1-11, P144:10-26: His giving rises on Friday nights, in Rajab, Sha'ban and Ramadan, so it is by choice, not a necessary outpouring), O.50 (JS P116, 27:40 "my Lord is rich and generous", one line at most), O.81 (JS U7.42-3 P084, the captive spared for forbearance and generosity), O.45's answer (Ibn Maytham, P158 to P165). [NEW (H.35, O.21, B.22, O.44, O.43, O.45, O.42, K.35, O.50, O.81); O.23 PART: recall B1 S16 "AH: giving only increases His generosity" then new: the attribute itself, nothing decreases from Him, by Nahj 91 and al-Iftitah, JS P054:5-21, P056:4-9; K.37 PART: recall B3 S07 "EZ: thanks brings increase (14:7)" then new: 3:26 read for His freedom, the more or less of bounty tied to thanks and ingratitude, EZ P145:4-14; B.21 PART: recall B3 S14 "AH: His bounty needs no receptivity; matter is clothed from an endless mine of perfections" then new: on the side of bestowing He is unlimited, P093:1-4, Teacher Copy]
  7. Close: then may the sinner hope? Yes: «ادْعُونِي أَسْتَجِبْ لَكُمْ», «لَا تَقْنَطُوا مِن رَّحْمَةِ اللَّهِ», and this is His own word in His own Book, so the servant pleads by it; whoever turns to Him is not turned back. One paragraph that hands on to the next lesson's question (if mercy is so wide, why disasters and Hell) and onward to pardon (S25) and dua (S27) (AH P039:1-8; O.19, AH half only; JS P042 and EZ P044, P045 to S27/S28, diag Q2). Re-read RR1-057 (ZH-S34 ¶123-127, "if I, a mean man, would forgive a tenth of this crowd, then You the Generous, where shall Your servants go?") recalled, not taught. [NEW (O.19); RR1-057 SAME: recall B1 S06 "AH: Lord, if a tenth of this gathering came to my door I would forgive them; You are the Generous: where shall we go?"]

Texts and Jami results

6:12 «كَتَبَ عَلَىٰ نَفْسِهِ الرَّحْمَةَ» (key ayah); the Mi'raj report «سبوح قدوس، أنا رب الملائكة والروح، سبقت رحمتي غضبي» (MM P001; Jami: al-Kafi via Sharh al-Kafi 7:153, Mir'at al-'uqul 5:200-201, graded «ضعيف»; wording as printed); «الله رحيم بعباده ومن رحمته أنه خلق مائة رحمة» (MM P007; Bihar 8:44, Tafsir al-Imam, as printed); «اللهم إني أسألك برحمتك التي وسعت كل شيء» (Du'a Kumayl, Iqbal 3:331, as printed); «ادخلوا الجنة برحمتي...» and «فإني لن أجزيكم اليوم بأعمالكم ولكن برحمتي وكرامتي وطولي» (MM P021, P023; al-Ikhtisas 356, Bihar 8:218; Bihar 8:153-154, as printed); «الحمد لله الذي لا يفره المنع والجمود ولا يكديه الإعطاء والجود» (Nahj 91; A'lam Nahj al-Balagha 1:90, Bihar 74:316, as printed); «فهو الجواد إن أعطى وإن منع» (JS P153, Imam Rida; Jami: only inside Ibn Maytham's Nahj commentary 2:326, no hadith collection); «إلهي أنعمت علي فلم تجدني شاكرا ... إلهي ما يكون من الكريم إلا الكرم» (JS P151; Bihar 96:197-198, Imam al-Hasan only, «وقيل»; print fixes «أنعمت علي», «فلا أنت سلبت», «أدمت»); the Rajab dua «يا من يعطي من سأله يا من يعطي من لم يسأله ومن لم يعرفه» (AH P022, JS P167; Iqbal 3, Zad al-ma'ad 16-17; print in Jami wording).

Next lesson picks up

اگر اس کی رحمت اتنی وسیع ہے تو دنیا میں آفتیں کیوں ہیں، اور جہنم کس لیے ہے؟ (If His mercy is so wide, why are there disasters, and what is Hell for?) (S24)

S24 خدا کی رحمت وسیع ہے مگر حکمت کے ساتھ چلتی ہے؛ اس کا غضب بھی اسی حکمت سے اس کا فعل ہے، اس میں کوئی تبدیلی نہیں، اس لیے بندہ خدا سے نہیں، اپنے گناہوں اور اس کے حضور کھڑے ہونے سے ڈرتا ہے اور امید بھی رکھتا ہے

ledger · 10 new · 5 part-taught before · 2 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. If mercy is so wide, why the tsunami, the plague, and Hell for a Shimr? Mercy is His act, not His Essence, and His act goes where wisdom calls: knowledge without ignorance and power without weakness are absolute, mercy is bounded by wisdom; no means and no pleading turns His wisdom to an unwise act (EZ U7.41-2 P077:12-25, calamities not to be laid on His account, human freedom; JS P069:7-20 the three-way objection and the one-sentence answer, P079:34-45 mercy is His act; JS U7.41-3 P087:5-6; the Sahifa line printed once in the Jami wording «يا من لا تبدل حكمته الوسائل», not the lecturer's «لا ترد / لا يرد / الوثائل»; the objection told without the Africa flight, ledger and diag Q9; B7.O.24, B7.O.27). new
  2. Then is His mercy limited? No end to how far it reaches (EZ: even the highest station still has room to rise, which is why a shafi' can help one above him), yet it does not go where wisdom does not call (JS: in the dua it is «واسعہ», not «بے حد»; better said "beyond our describing"). Two sides of one thing, reach and application, reconciled in one sentence with no "contradiction" label (JS P094:1-15, P096:2-8; EZ P100:13-15, P100:6-12 one line; B7.O.26; diag Q4). new
  3. Can one and the same act be mercy for some and punishment for others? Yes: asked about the plague, Imam al-Sadiq said it is God's punishment for a people and mercy for others, as the one Fire is punishment for the unbelievers and mercy for its keepers; so mercy and wrath are His two acts, each given by His full wisdom to the group it fits, and the Quran draws the line by thanks and ingratitude (MM U7.41-3 P121:10-13, key text, 'Uyun; EZ U7.41-2 P091:9-14 with 14:7 inline only (its full print is in S26); JS P090:10-18 «ورحمتي وسعت كل شيء» (7:156) and "His punishment is severe" in one line; B7.O.11, B7.O.25, its mercy-and-wrath half; the eternal-punishment half goes to step 7). new
  4. What is His wrath, then? Not a change of state in Him but His punishment; His hatred is His punishment (MM's own sentence, given in Urdu only, not as Arabic); His severity on His enemies stands inside the breadth of His mercy, and His mercy for His friends inside the severity of His vengeance; and He is roused for the right of the wronged: when al-Husayn comes with his veins flowing and asks for his right, «فيغضب عند ذلك الجليل» (MM U7.41-3 P123:1-3 meaning only; JS U7.41-2 P063:2-9, Nahj 90, cut after the first two clauses, with JS's plain reading P064:3-9; MM U7.41-3 P124:1-4, only the wrath clause, the rest of the Fatima minbar report Teacher Copy; EZ P065:2-4 the curse ayat 33:57 and 2:159 fine layer; B7.O.13 without the 2:15 mockery part, B7.O.54, B7.O.14). new The AH re-read RR4-028 (no hesitation or change in God) is recalled here, SAME.
  5. How do Generous and Wise go together? He is the most merciful of the merciful in the place of pardon and mercy and the severest of punishers in the place of punishment; He is Generous, and also Wise, so if wisdom calls for taking a man to account His generosity stays whole and the account still comes; «ما غرك بربك الكريم» is a warning, not a licence (JS U7.41-2 P101:7-12, the Iftitah line completed from Jami «... في موضع النكال والنقمة»; JS U7.41-3 P131:2-11, 82:6; B7.O.29, B7.O.28). [O.29 PART: recall B1 S03 "the Iftitah line, printed there for the degrees of the testimony" then new: the same line read as His mercy and severity as attributes, in JS P101:7-11; O.28 NEW]
  6. So what should the servant fear? Not God as one fears a lion or a bomb: He is the most merciful of the merciful, kinder than a mother, and the Quran opens with Rahman and Rahim; fear standing before your Lord (79:40) and your own sins; the Quran holds the servant between fear and hope (15:49-50); even Jibra'il wept for fear of the Fire, and God sent word to him and the Prophet that He had given them safety (EZ U7.41-5 P185:4-8, P190:2-5, P190:8-18 (79:40); EZ U7.41-2 P066:4-13 (15:49-50 once); MM U7.41-3 P121:4-5 (only with «فوالله ما» restored) and P121:1; B7.O.112, B7.O.114, B7.O.10; B7.O.118 "God is a friend, Satan the enemy" (35:6) Teacher Copy only). [O.112 PART: recall B1 S18 "do not paint God as one to be dreaded, from the bismillah on" then new: the Fatiha's fourfold mercy set against a wrathful God, in EZ P185:4-8 (the Torah contrast an ASK); O.114 PART: recall B1 S18 then new: fear one's sins and the standing, khashiyat from His majesty is not dread of Him, in EZ P190:2-18; O.10 PART (verdict changed, see Asks): recall B1 S07 "the angels' fear of the Fire, Jibra'il no longer smiling, «جفت لي عين», and the safety message, all MM" then new: the safety message read for this step, fear and safety together; O.118 NEW, TC]
  7. (Only if Ali keeps it at M1; diag recommends Teacher Copy) Is eternal punishment against mercy? No, if wisdom calls for it: khulud is only for the obstinate; every punishment rests on the servant's own choice; they would return to it if sent back (6:28, after 35:37); and those who said the Fire turns sweet («عذاب» becomes «عذب») are answered: there is khulud and there is punishment (JS U7.41-2 P090:1-18; JS U7.41-4 P136:1-2, P139:1-4, P140:1-4, P136:4-5; JS U7.41-3 P134:4-10, 26-27; P134:11-19 in one line, no names; B7.O.25, the eternal half, B7.O.33, B7.O.34, B7.B.19). [O.33 PART: recall B1 S17 and B1 S15 "JS against the view that the people of Hell end content, so that Hell is denied" then new: the "punishment turns sweet" claim answered from mercy and justice, in JS P134:16-27; O.34 and B.19 SAME: recall only]

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «عذاب الله لقوم ورحمة لآخرين، قالوا: وكيف تكون الرحمة عذابا؟ قال: أما تعرفون أن نيران جهنم عذاب على الكفار وخزنة جهنم معهم فيها فهي رحمة عليهم» (Imam al-Sadiq on the plague, 'Uyun akhbar al-Rida via al-'Askari; Bihar 6:121; Jami FOUND, wording as printed).
  2. «هو الذي اشتدت نقمته على أعدائه في سعة رحمته، واتسعت رحمته لأوليائه في شدة نقمته» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj sermon 90; Bihar 4:310-311; Jami FOUND), cut after the two clauses.
  3. «أرحم الراحمين في موضع العفو والرحمة وأشد المعاقبين في موضع النكال والنقمة» (Du'a al-Iftitah; Misbah al-mutahajjid 578-579; Jami FOUND; the printed excerpt stops at «في موضع» and must be completed).

  6. «فيغضب عند ذلك الجليل ويغضب لغضبه جهنم والملائكة أجمعون» (the Prophet to Sayyida Fatima, Tafsir Furat; Bihar 8:54-55; Jami FOUND).
  7. «فوالله ما جفت لي عين منذ خلق الله النار ...» and «يا جبرئيل ويا محمد إن الله قد آمنكما من أن تعصيا فيعذبكما» (Jibra'il, Bihar 56:260, an unsourced «روي»; the message: al-Duru' al-waqiya 1:267-273, Bihar 8:306 «من أن تعصياه»; Jami FOUND); 79:40 and 15:49-50 inline.
Next lesson picks up

اگر اس کا غضب حقیقی ہے اور بندہ اپنے گناہوں سے ڈرتا ہے، تو گناہگار کہاں جائے: کیا اس کا عفو ہمارے گناہوں سے بڑا ہے، اور جب بندہ لوٹتا ہے تو وہ اس سے کیا کرتا ہے؟ (S25)

S25 اس کا عفو ہمارے گناہوں سے بڑا ہے: بندہ پشیمان ہو کر لوٹے تو اللہ بھی اس کی طرف لوٹتا ہے، اس کے عیب ڈھانپتا ہے اور اس کی برائیوں کو نیکیوں میں بدل دیتا ہے

ledger H25 · 25 new · 2 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 7
  1. What do maghfira and 'afw mean? Maghfira is covering the sin, 'afw is wiping it out; the teacher who covers the mistake on the paper and the one who tears the page; one dua joins the two, «فإنك مليء بالعفو مرجو للمغفرة». EZ and JS rank them in opposite orders: the entry gives both and the reconciliation Ali approves. (EZ U7.42-1 P037:1-10, P036, P018:1-16; JS P035:8-16; B7.O.57). [B7.O.57 NEW]
  2. Must the sinner pay back the years of sin? No: sin is not measured by time or place; sincere regret is repentance, «إن كان الندم على الذنب توبة فإني وعزتك من النادمين», and none forgives sins but God (3:135). (EZ P002:1-21, P002:32-33; JS P001:8-14, P001:26; B7.O.60). [B7.O.60 PART: recall B4 S01 "EZ: one who was a mushrik sixty or seventy years and says the shahada owes no qada and no kaffara", then new: regret itself is repentance, sin not measured by time (the one-minute murder) or place (wine in the mosque), the Munajat al-Ta'ibin line and 3:135, in EZ P002:1-21, P002:32-33 and JS P001:8-14]
  3. What does He do when the servant turns? It is His sunna: when the servant turns to Him He turns to the servant; He rejoices at it as one who finds his lost mount; He pardons generously without shaming; He covers the penitent even from the two angels, his own limbs and the ground; He is Sattar al-'uyub, «يا من أظهر الجميل وستر القبيح». The door is shut to none, not even the warrior and the apostate who repent (5:33-34), and the angels' pity for man between self and Satan is answered by His forgiveness. (JS U7.42-2 P063:5-8; JS P064:15, one Kafi report; JS U7.42-1 P038:5-16, told once; JS P039 tawba nasuh, one block; EZ P040, P041, P043:15 Kumayl, one line; EZ U7.42-2 P061:8-22, P062:10-12; JS U7.41-2 P108:1-11, EZ P110:1-11; JS U7.41-4 P145:6-14, P146, P147:7-23; B7.O.59, O.62, O.58, O.84, O.38, O.40; Teacher Copy fine layer O.80, O.83, O.128, O.119). [B7.O.59 NEW | B7.O.62 NEW | B7.O.58 NEW | B7.O.84 PART: recall B3 S05 "EZ's thought on «يا من أظهر الجميل وستر القبيح»: a skin less than a millimetre thick drawn over pus and filth, there as His fine work in creation", then new: the same dua read as His covering of our sins, "if our sins were laid open all would be ruined", and the coverer of faults as the friend He loves most, in EZ U7.42-2 P061:8-10, P061:18-22, P062:10-12 | B7.O.38 NEW | B7.O.40 NEW | B7.O.80 NEW (Teacher Copy, one line) | B7.O.83 NEW (Teacher Copy) | B7.O.128 NEW (Teacher Copy note) | B7.O.119 NEW (Teacher Copy)]
  4. And if the sins are many or great? «عفو الله أكثر»: His pardon is more; He changes the repented sins into good deeds (25:70); «وعُصي فغفر», He was disobeyed and forgave, and He smooths every pothole of the road (39:53); He forgives all sins, not all sinners; He has set countless means of forgiveness, and a bad intention not acted on is not written; avoid the great sins and the slips are forgiven (53:32, 4:31); «يا من يقبل اليسير ويعفو عن الكثير». (JS U7.42-1 P024:11-20; JS P022:6-9, P022:10 block, P023:13-26; JS U7.41-1 P046:2-16; EZ P047:1-18; EZ U7.42-3 P093:1-2, P094:5-14; JS U7.41-4 P143:5-12, P143:54-65; EZ U7.42-3 P096; EZ P095, dua line only; B7.O.85, O.79, O.20, O.125, O.37, O.127, O.126; Teacher Copy O.73). [B7.O.85 NEW | B7.O.79 NEW | B7.O.20 NEW | B7.O.125 NEW | B7.O.37 NEW | B7.O.127 NEW (one line) | B7.O.126 NEW (Teacher Copy, one line) | B7.O.73 NEW (Teacher Copy) | B7.O.124 NEW (Teacher Copy note: EZ U7.42-3 P092 asks whether God is Ghaffar al-dhunub or reward a causal result of deeds, unanswered, Ask 20)]
  5. Is acceptance then certain? Forgiving is His right, even without istighfar, and accepting one repentance and not another wrongs no one, for repentance is grace, not desert; He has promised to accept a sincere repentance, yet the servant cannot be sure his own is complete, so he lives between fear and hope. (JS U7.42-1 P047; EZ P048, P057; EZ P050:2, P051:6-14, P053; JS P016:4-8, P049:9-11, P052:2-8; B7.O.61, O.67). [B7.O.61 NEW | B7.O.67 NEW]
  6. What stands in the way of repentance? Despair (the man in tawaf who thought his sins too many), Satan's ruse of delay, and belittling a small sin: God loves the one who asks Him in a great crime and hates the one who makes light of a small sin. (EZ U7.42-1 P025, two sentences; JS U7.42-2 P074:1-9, P074:42-47, cut to the Iblis part; JS P076:8-12; B7.O.65, O.68; despair is O.85's story). [B7.O.65 NEW | B7.O.68 NEW]
  7. How does the penitent speak to Him? «إلهي ما أنا بأول من عصاك فتبت عليه»; to ask pardon while persisting is baseness and to leave istighfar is weakness; and he comes as Imam Hasan came to the mosque door: «يا محسن قد أتاك المسيء». (JS U7.42-2 P068, P069; JS P070:2-8, one line each; JS U7.42-1 P020, P054; AH RR4-010; B7.O.63, O.71). [B7.O.63 NEW | B7.O.71 NEW (the dua's Arabic is already printed in full in B1 S18, there for the knower's awe at the door; reprinted, flagged)]

AH re-read adds 43
  • Imports (S25 is a thin lesson; an import is Teacher Copy only and never the ground of a step; U10.11 is Bab 10's "God's ways with His servants", almost all on guidance, tawfiq, sovereignty and respite):
    • U10.11.8 (MM; al-Tusi's Amali report quoted by MM: a servant pleads in the fire seventy and seventy autumns by the right of Muhammad and his family, and God tells Jibril to bring him out, the fire made cool for him): kept, Teacher Copy under step 4 beside O.73. Claude's judgement MM brings the report among texts on the fire as punishment for some and mercy for others; framed here, it shows from the report's own words that even one already in the fire for ages is pardoned when he turns and pleads, which supports step 4 (no sin, however great, is beyond His pardon). Report unverified (gather hint: al-Amali of al-Tusi 533 no. 1162, not checked); pleading by the right of Muhammad is used in B1 for another claim (U1.16.61).
    • U10.11.1 (MM, EZ, JS; knowledge and faith are God's making, al-Baqir's "until God is their teacher"): Dropped: no sound framing. Guidance as His act is not a step of a lesson on pardon; base already in B1 S06.
    • U10.11.2 (MM; hadith qudsi "all of you are astray except whom I guide ... all of you are sinners except whom I protect"): Dropped: no sound framing. MM uses it for guidance as His act; the "sinners" clause is about 'isma, not pardon, and would add a voice only.
    • U10.11.3 (MM; 6:46, if God took guidance who would restore it): Dropped: no sound framing; guidance, not pardon.
    • U10.11.4 (MM, AH; hearts between two of His fingers; AH: all things turned over, hope in and fear of His "whom He wills"): Dropped: no sound framing. AH's fear and hope are about His giving and taking wealth and knowledge, not about acceptance of repentance (step 5); using it there would only add a voice.
    • U10.11.5 (MM; the white spot in the heart and the angel): Dropped: no sound framing; tawfiq of the heart; its Arabic is already printed in full in B1 S05.

    • U10.11.11 (AH; every motion and stillness His act): Dropped: no sound framing; power in nature.
    • U10.11.12 (AH; Pharaoh and the birth of Moses): Dropped: no sound framing; SAME as B3 (H11), decree, not pardon.

  1. ﴿وَمَن يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ إِلَّا اللَّهُ﴾ (3:135, JS U7.42-1 P001:8): inline, Tanzil wording (the lesson has «لا يغفر الذنوب الا الله»; fix).
  2. «إِنْ كَانَ النَّدَمُ عَلَى الذَّنْبِ تَوْبَةً فَإِنِّي وَعِزَّتِكَ مِنَ النَّادِمِينَ» (EZ P002:18): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Munajat al-Ta'ibin of Imam al-Sajjad (al-Sahifa supplement 293-296; Bihar 91:142); print «الندم» and «من النادمين» as Jami has them; name the speaker.
  3. «إِنَّ اللَّهَ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ يَفْرَحُ بِتَوْبَةِ عَبْدِهِ الْمُؤْمِنِ إِذَا تَابَ كَمَا يَفْرَحُ أَحَدُكُمْ بِضَالَّتِهِ إِذَا وَجَدَهَا» (JS U7.42-2 P064:15): FOUND, al-Kafi 2:411; cut «عن أبي عبد الله ع» from the Arabic. The second report (P064:21, al-Kafi 2:410) FOUND; one of the two (ASK).
  4. «أَحَبَّهُ اللَّهُ فَسَتَرَ عَلَيْهِ فِي الدُّنْيَا وَالْآخِرَةِ ... يُنْسِي مَلَكَيْهِ ... وَيُوحِي إِلَى جَوَارِحِهِ اكْتُمِي عَلَيْهِ ذُنُوبَهُ» (JS U7.42-1 P039): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, al-Kafi 2:407-408 (Mu'awiya b. Wahb); one block in Jami's wording, not four fragments.
  5. «عَفْوُ اللَّهِ أَكْثَرُ» (JS P024:11): FOUND, the Prophet to Habib b. al-Harith (Mishkat al-Anwar 216; Wasa'il from al-Daylami's Irshad); name the speaker.
  6. ﴿إِلَّا مَن تَابَ وَآمَنَ وَعَمِلَ عَمَلًا صَالِحًا فَأُولَٰئِكَ يُبَدِّلُ اللَّهُ سَيِّئَاتِهِمْ حَسَنَاتٍ﴾ (25:70, JS P022:10): block, key verse; ﴿قُلْ يَا عِبَادِيَ الَّذِينَ أَسْرَفُوا ... إِنَّ اللَّهَ يَغْفِرُ الذُّنُوبَ جَمِيعًا﴾ (39:53, EZ U7.42-3 P093:1) once, in EZ's "sins, not sinners" reading (also EZ U7.41-1 P047:14-16; inline in B1 S02, B4 S01).
  7. «إِلَهِي مَا أَنَا بِأَوَّلِ مَنْ عَصَاكَ فَتُبْتَ عَلَيْهِ» (JS U7.42-2 P068:5): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Munajat al-Ta'ibin (Zad al-Ma'ad 407); print «عليه», not «علي». Others in the steps: «إلهي ضيفك ببابك يا محسن قد أتاك المسيء ...» FOUND (Manaqib Ibn Shahrashub p. 143; Misbah al-Mutahajjid 30), reprinted flagged; Kumayl «أمرت بإثباتها الكرام الكاتبين» FOUND (Misbah al-Mutahajjid; Jamal al-Usbu' 550-551), restore «وكل سيئة»; «فإنك مليء بالعفو مرجو للمغفرة معروف بالتجاوز» FOUND (al-Misbah 387), print «مرجو»; «يا من أظهر الجميل وستر القبيح» FOUND (Iqbal; Mustadrak Safina 3:309); «إن الله يحب العبد أن يطلب إليه في الجرم العظيم ويبغض العبد أن يستخف بالجرم اليسير» FOUND, Imam al-Sadiq (Idah al-Dala'il 302), Majlisi's name out; «اللهم إن الاستغفار مع الإصرار لؤم وتركي الاستغفار مع معرفتي بكرمك عجز» FOUND-OTHER-WORDING (Ikhtiyar al-Misbah 2:39; Bihar 91:132 «مع سعة رحمتك»), the printed «مع معرفتي بسعة جودك ورحمتك» is not found; «اللهم إن عظم الذنب من عبدك فليحسن العفو من عندك» FOUND-OTHER-WORDING (Iqbal), restore «إن عظم» (P001:26); «الإسلام يجب ما قبله» (EZ P002:23) in Jami only in a Sunni gharib book, no Shi'i source; «يا من يقبل اليسير ويعفو عن الكثير» FOUND (Amali al-Mufid majlis 34; Mustadrak al-Wasa'il 2:128-129), print the dua, not the vision; «وتبث عليه أمرا قد ستره الله عليه» FOUND (al-Kafi 2); «يا محب التوابين» FOUND (Muhaj al-Da'awat 444); «إلهي أفكر في عفوك ...» FOUND-OTHER-WORDING (Rawdat al-Wa'izin 261); «فإن ترحم فأنت لذاك» POEM, «منسوب»; 5:33-34, 53:32, 4:31 Tanzil.
Next lesson picks up

اللہ اپنا وعدہ تو ضرور پورا کرتا ہے؛ کیا وعید بھی ضرور پوری کرتا ہے، اور بندوں کے حق، اس کے حلم اور ظالموں کو مہلت کا کیا معاملہ ہے؟ (He surely keeps His promise; must He also carry out His threat, and what of people's rights, His forbearance and the respite He gives wrongdoers?) (S26)

S26 خدا اپنا وعدہ کبھی نہیں توڑتا اور اپنی وعید معاف کر سکتا ہے؛ وہ اپنا حق بخشتا ہے مگر لوگوں کا حق ان کے لیے لیتا ہے، اور اس کا حلم اور ظالموں کو مہلت دینا قدرت کے ہوتے ہوئے ہے، کمزوری نہیں، اس لیے اس کے غضب سے بے خوف نہیں رہا جا سکتا

ledger · 9 new · 3 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. Does He treat promise and threat alike? No: He never goes back on a promise, and He may forgo a threat as grace; "He who is true to His promise"; the promise of the Gardens of Eden to those who believed unseen will surely come (JS U7.42-1 P003:4-8, P004:4-7 with «الذي صدق في ميعاده» inline; the line «يا من إذا وعد وفى وإذا توعد عفا» printed once, full, in the Jami wording (Iqbal, Ikhtiyar al-Misbah), with the speaker as Jami gives it; JS P016:32-39 one line; JS P008:1-9 (19:61); B7.O.66). The equal-scales case (those whose good and bad weigh alike are left to the Most Merciful's decision) as a Teacher Copy note, the Asfar passage in Urdu only (JS U7.41-4 P142:1-11; B7.O.35). [O.66 NEW; O.35 NEW, TC]
  2. Is any of His great promises unconditioned? One: among forgiveness, enriching, answering, provision and accepting repentance, each carrying "if He wills" (4:48, 9:28, 6:41), only the increase for thanks is promised outright: «لئن شكرتم لأزيدنكم» (JS U7.41-4 P173:1-15, P175:21-27, P176:13-36, P179:1-19; P178:27-34 the increase comes in the thing thanked for; B7.O.49). [PART: recall B3 S07 "thanks brings increase by 14:7 (EZ, with JS)" then new: JS's point that this one promise alone has no «إن شاء», in JS P173:7-15, P179:10-19] The AH re-read RR3-022 (14:7 agrees with every conscience, the two beggars) and RR4-033 (blessings withheld by our sins, 7:96, the five fields) are recalled here, SAME.
  3. Then is every sin simply wiped out? His own right He forgives, even on the day of Arafa; but He does not tell you to keep what you took: "I forgave your sin; go and pay people their right" (the double line and the traffic fines waived that day: the fine is gone, the damage is still owed); sins are of three kinds (EZ U7.42-1 P018:17-19, P019:4-42; P055:127-129 told in Urdu, named from Jami as Amir al-Mu'minin «إن الذنوب ثلاثة»; EZ P053:2-4 hadd and qisas in one line; B7.O.69). new The AH re-read RR3-025 (a realm of balancing must take the oppressed one's right) is recalled here, SAME; RR5-006 (a hadd here spares the punishment there), NONE, Teacher Copy only, an Ask.
  4. And if the right can no longer be returned? Ask Him to satisfy the claimant: "my hand fell short ... and that You satisfy him on my behalf as You will"; do some good on the wronged one's behalf; He can make him content, as a father pays the son's creditor more than the debt (EZ U7.42-1 P055:1-7, P055:45-50; the Sahifa Monday du'a «فقصرت يدي ... وأن ترضيه عني بما شئت» from Jami; one example only, EZ P057:12-32 or JS P056:18-29; JS P054:4-15 one line if the du'a before prayer is found in Jami; B7.O.70). new
  5. Why does He not punish at once? Forbearance: His forbearance is great, so He pardoned, and He was just in all He decreed; pardon is not weakness, since only one able to punish can pardon; He does not hasten because He does not fear that anything will slip from Him; He gives the sinner time so that he may exchange disobedience for return; and He forbore their bad deeds by His prior knowledge of what they would do (JS U7.42-1 P026:4-5, P026:19-21, block «عظم حلمه فعفا وعدل في كل ما قضى»; JS P027:1-11; JS U7.41-2 P059:4-18 «يا من لا يعجل لأنه لا يخاف الفوت»; JS P058:3-14, P061:5-10, Sahifa 37 «لكي يستبدل بحاله في معصيتك حال الإنابة إلى طاعتك»; MM U7.12-2 P139:5, al-Sadiq to Hafs, with «فلا يغرنك حسن الطلب ممن لا يخاف الفوت»; EZ U7.41-2 P062:6-7 one line, the Ibn Muljam frame an Ask; MM's "Allahu akbar as forbearance" (MMB_04_S03 ¶95) only if gathered; B7.E.28, B7.O.53). new
  6. Even with wrongdoers who go on? Yes: the greatest instance of His forbearance is Ashura, the men killed and the women taken captive, and He did not halt it by a miracle; the believers of the trench were burned for nothing but believing in the Mighty, the Praised, who owns the heavens and the earth and witnesses all; this is the sunna of respite, and the servant takes refuge in His power that nothing resists (JS U7.42-1 P026:6-16, P030:1-10 (wording an Ask); JS U7.42-3 P090:25-33, P091:2-27 (85:8-9), P090:14-24, P090:1-7 one line; 3:178 once, JS P090:34-35; B7.O.89, B7.O.90). [O.89 PART (verdict changed, see Asks): recall B3 S19 "the sunna of respite and 3:178: the respite the unbelievers get is not their good" then new: His forbearance at Ashura as its greatest instance, in JS P026:6-16, P030:1-10, and EZ's istidraj in step 7; O.90 PART: recall B3 S19 "why God does not stop tyrants who massacre: the sunna of respite, the people of the trench" (B4 S17 used the trench for tawhid) then new: the sunna taught at its root, the names Mighty and Praised, refuge in His unstoppable power, in JS P090:25-33, P091:2-27]
  7. Does respite prove them right, or make Him unjust? Neither: taking ease as proof that one is right is the error of istidraj: He leads them on step by step and gives them rope, then seizes them (7:182-183); He never wrongs anyone; why a kind God gives them plenty is answered in one line (the lam of «ليزدادوا» marks the outcome, not His aim) or the question is dropped (EZ U7.42-1 P031:9-44, P031:46-49 without «ولا يخاف الظلم», P032:27-29; JS P033:9-22 if Ali approves the line; B7.O.89, its istidraj half). The AH re-read RR5-019 (the man at Ghadir killed by a stone, 70:1) as a Teacher Copy sample of His swift seizing, PART, an Ask.
  8. So may we feel safe? No: His wrath is real; Bal'am was given His signs and rose high, then followed his desire and fell until his likeness was a dog's; "when I am obeyed I am pleased, when pleased I bless and My blessing has no end; when disobeyed I am angry, and when angry I curse"; and He has set shields against His anger, goodness to parents among them (JS U7.42-3 P088:1-29; JS P089:1-4, the Kafi block with the passive «أُطِعْتُ ... عُصِيتُ»; JS P087:2-10, the Fadak line «وبر الوالدين وقاية من السخط» with Sayyida Fatima named, Majlisi's name dropped; B7.O.87, B7.O.88, B7.O.86). new
Asks for Ali 25

Arabic printed again

«وإذ تأذن ربكم لئن شكرتم لأزيدنكم ولئن كفرتم إن عذابي لشديد» (14:7; already printed in full in B1 S16, B2 S02, B3 S07; step 2; the Bab's one full print, S24 quotes it inline); «إن الله لا يغفر أن يشرك به ويغفر ما دون ذلك لمن يشاء» (4:48; already printed in full in B2 S10, B4 S01; step 2); «بل إياه تدعون فيكشف ما تدعون إليه إن شاء» (6:41; already printed in full in B2 S10; inline here; step 2); «الذي صدق في ميعاده» (Nahj; already printed in full in B3 S03, inside its sermon block; inline here; step 1); «ولا يحسبن الذين كفروا أنما نملي لهم خير لأنفسهم إنما نملي لهم ليزدادوا إثما» (3:178; already printed in full in B3 S19, the same JS reading; step 6); «وما نقموا منهم إلا أن يؤمنوا بالله العزيز الحميد» (85:8; already printed in full in B4 S17, inline in B3 S19; step 6). Checked and not counted: «الحمد لله الفاشي في الخلق حمده ...» (table: printed in full B5 S04) is the long Nahj sermon; B5 S04 printed only its opening line, and this lesson's block «عظم حلمه فعفا وعدل في كل ما قضى» is not in any B1 to B6 lesson; the Thawab al-A'mal matn matched at U7.42-3 P090:3-7 (table: B4 S12) is a short shared run, and the P090:8 du'a against pain is an Ask to drop.

Who carries it

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «يا من إذا وعد وفى وإذا توعد عفا» (Iqbal al-A'mal; Ikhtiyar al-Misbah; Majma' al-Bahrayn 3:162 «في الدعاء»; Jami FOUND-OTHER-WORDING: H1 prints only the first half, H2 «وأوعد فعفا»; print the full line from Jami). With 19:61 «جنات عدن التي وعد الرحمن عباده بالغيب».
  2. «وإذ تأذن ربكم لئن شكرتم لأزيدنكم ولئن كفرتم إن عذابي لشديد» (14:7), against 4:48 and 6:41.
  3. «فقصرت يدي ... وأن ترضيه عني بما شئت» (Imam al-Sajjad, Sahifa, Monday du'a; Jami FOUND).
  4. «عظم حلمه فعفا وعدل في كل ما قضى» (Amir al-Mu'minin, Nahj; Ibn Maytham 4:211-212; Jami FOUND), with «يا من لا يعجل لأنه لا يخاف الفوت» (a du'a the Prophet taught, Muhaj al-da'awat 101, Bihar 91:214; Jami FOUND; the scholar tags it Imam al-Sajjad) and «إن الله تبارك وتعالى علم ما العباد عليه عاملون وإلى ما هم صائرون فحلم عنهم ...» (Imam al-Sadiq to Hafs, Tafsir al-Qummi; Bihar 2:27; Jami FOUND).
  5. «وما نقموا منهم إلا أن يؤمنوا بالله العزيز الحميد، الذي له ملك السماوات والأرض والله على كل شيء شهيد» (85:8-9), with 3:178 once.
  6. «والذين كذبوا بآياتنا سنستدرجهم من حيث لا يعلمون وأملي لهم إن كيدي متين» (7:182-183).
  7. «أوحى الله عز وجل إلى نبي من الأنبياء: إذا أُطِعْتُ رضيت، وإذا رضيت باركت، وليس لبركتي نهاية، وإذا عُصِيتُ غضبت، وإذا غضبت لعنت ...» (Imam al-Rida, al-Kafi 2:275; Bihar 70:341; Jami FOUND; vocalisation to passive), with «وبر الوالدين وقاية من السخط» (Sayyida Fatima, the Fadak sermon, al-Ihtijaj 259; Jami FOUND).
Next lesson picks up

جب بندہ اپنے رب کو وعدہ پورا کرنے والا، حلیم اور غضب والا پہچان لے تو وہ اس سے کیسے مانگے: دعا کیا ہے، اور مانگنے کا سلیقہ کیا ہے؟ (S27)

9. Calling on Him: dua, praise, thanks S27 to S28

S27 جو سب کچھ جانتا، سب کچھ کر سکتا اور سب سے بڑھ کر مہربان ہے، اسی سے مانگا جائے، اسی کی پناہ لی جائے اور اسی پر بھروسا کیا جائے

ledger · 27 new · 8 part-taught before · 6 taught before · 0 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. What is dua, and why does it matter to Him? Contact with the Lord, as remembrance or as request; His care for us hangs on our calling (25:77, block once); the one absorbed in His remembrance is given more than the asker (JS H1 P013:4-11, H2 P035:1-2; JS U7.14-2 P066 with Jami's full ending «أفضل ما أعطي من سألني»; B7.O.91, B7.O.94, B7.E.74). [O.91 PART: recall B5 S04 "dhikr is without request, du'a with request" then new: du'a as contact covering both, praise before asking (JS H1 P013:5-11, P014); O.94 NEW; E.74 PART: recall B1 S16 "the state of supplication is worth more than what is asked" then new: the hadith qudsi «من شغل بذكري عن مسألتي» (JS U7.14-2 P066)]
  2. Why does reason see its worth? The needy, ignorant and weak turn to the One who is Rich, Knowing and Powerful without limit; JS's triad said once here as the Fasl's ground and pointed back to later (JS H1 P017:4-8, P014:4-8, P034:5-7). [NEW for the Fasl's own wording; the AH side (RR4-007, RR4-008, RR2-017) SAME: recall only]
  3. Does He hear and answer? He said «ادْعُونِي أَسْتَجِبْ لَكُمْ» and «أُجِيبُ دَعْوَةَ الدَّاعِ إِذَا دَعَانِ»; He hears every prayer and does not turn the asker away, so keep asking; a withheld prayer has its reason (EZ H3 P065:58-66; JS H1 P003:2-3; EZ H1 P004 with 3:26 once; EZ H1 P006:4-6 one line only; B7.O.98, B7.O.120 fine layer). AH re-reads under it: RR1-027, RR5-004, RR4-022 (27:62 in his closing duas), RR5-021, RR2-010, RR1-026, RR1-058, RR1-048, RR1-020, RR1-022, RR5-022. [O.98 PART: recall B1 S12 "a withheld prayer has its reason" then new: He hears every prayer, rejects no asker, keep asking (JS H1 P003:2-3, EZ H1 P004); the ayat base PART too: recall B1 S18 "He is near and answers the caller, 2:186 and 40:60 (EZ)" then new: the ayat read as the ground of asking Him, with JS's reason from His kindness, power and knowledge; O.120 NEW, Teacher Copy]
  4. How should one ask? Praise Him by His names first, then thanks, istighfar, salawat, then the request; good in both worlds (2:201); do not break the hope of one who hopes (JS H1 P022 block #3 names only, the Imam named from Jami as Imam Hasan al-'Askari; P023:1-19 with 38:35; JS U7.41-4 P166, P172, P181; EZ U7.12-5 P208:7, P222; B7.O.46, O.48, O.51, E.79, E.88, H.51; fine layer: E.22, E.81, A.55, A.57, A.58, O.41, O.64, O.74, A.59, A.60, A.56). [all NONE except A.56 PART: recall B4 S18 "Ali's dotless sermon on tawhid (EZ)" then new: the 25 years off the pulpit and the sermon without alif, Teacher Copy only]
  5. Why seek refuge with Him alone? Only one who knows, can and is kind gives refuge, and only He has all three without limit; the Prophet himself was commanded to seek refuge (23:97-98 block); «إنّ من تقه يسلم» (JS H2 P038:4-5, P039:3-14, P040:1-5, P046:6; 114:1 one line; B7.O.96; B7.O.77 fine layer). [NEW; RR4-018 SAME: recall only]
  6. What is tawakkul, and does it mean sitting idle? He suffices whoever relies on Him (65:3 with Imam al-Sadiq's «من أعطي التوكل أعطي الكفاية»), the Living who never sleeps does not leave him, so he looks to no one's hand; and he still uses the means He made (JS H2 P042:1-4, H1 P001:5-16, H1 P002:5-8 merged; JS H2 P039:14-19 with H1 P032:1-2 merged; EZ U7.1-1 P043, U7.1-2 P049 one example; 3:173 one line; B7.O.97, B7.A.22, B7.A.50; B7.O.100 Teacher Copy). [NEW; 2:255 recalled in one line to its home in this Bab (H11), not printed here; RR3-031 SAME: recall only]
  7. Who can count on Him most? Whoever obeys Him: «من كان في طاعة الله كان الله في حاجته» (Imam Hasan al-'Askari; JS H2 P047:1-20, block #13 with «حاجته» restored; B7.O.99). new
  8. Close: is dua only a means to an end? It is the prophets' weapon and is itself worship, so never neglect it (JS H2 P037:1-8 block #10, al-Kafi 2:439-440; «إن الدعاء أنفذ من السنان» from al-Kafi 2:440; JS H1 P030:1-5, P028:4, P019, P031; the helper argument P028:7-14 said once, at step 5; B7.O.95, B7.O.92). new

Next lesson picks up

اگر وہ ہر پکارنے والے کو جواب دیتا ہے تو کچھ دعائیں پوری کیوں نہیں ہوتیں، اور بندہ پھر کیا کہے؟ (S28)

S28 اللہ ہر پکارنے والے کو جواب دیتا ہے مگر وہی کرتا ہے جو ہماری بھلائی ہے؛ اس لیے دیر پر شکوہ نہیں، بلکہ وہ دعائیں جن کی حد نہیں، حمد، شکر اور صبر، اور اس سے رابطہ خود بندے کا فخر ہے

ledger H28 · 14 new · 7 part-taught before · 0 taught before · 1 kept in full
The steps 8
  1. If He answers every caller, why are some duas not fulfilled? He answers every call, "yes", once, twice, three times; but answering is not doing every wish: much of what we ask would harm us ("O God, give me death"), and the wise and kind One prefers our good to our wish, as a caring physician prefers the patient's health to his craving. (EZ H3 P064:2-18, P066:1-2; JS H2 P048:13 "the withheld answer is stored" once; B7.O.129; Teacher Copy: B7.K.30 (EZ U7.24 P111, P112, P117; JS P110, P113 to P118) and B7.H.42 (JS U7.21-4 P207, healing "unless death is set")). [B7.O.129 core repeat kept in full: base recalled "B1 S12: God sometimes withholds an answer to protect the asker (EZ, the sayyid's prayers)" | B7.K.30 PART: base recalled, new part Teacher Copy | B7.H.42 NEW, Teacher Copy pending re-gather]
  2. What should the servant say then? What Du'a al-Iftitah teaches: after His pardon and His covering of my sins I call Him safe and at ease, and if the answer is slow I complain out of ignorance; then «وَلَعَلَّ الَّذِي أَبْطَأَ عَنِّي هُوَ خَيْرٌ لِي لِعِلْمِكَ بِعَاقِبَةِ الْأُمُورِ»; so do not complain much when a dua is not answered: we do not know our children's future, a voice, a face, fame, even knowledge can be a calamity. (EZ H3 P066:3-8, P067:29 as block #19 in Jami wording, P067:3-21, P067:24-26 «إنك تدعوني فأولي عنك» one line, P067:37-38; still B7.O.129). [B7.O.129, as step 1]
  3. And when our own wishes clash? What we asked as a child (a good end) and what we ask today (an unfit spouse) can clash, and so can the wishes of different people (the young man and the girl's mother at the Ka'ba); then God is neither unjust nor stingy, and His hand is not tied; so ask «افعل بنا ما أنت أهله ولا تفعل بنا ما نحن أهله», and keep asking: every asking brings a reward. (EZ H3 P073:1-28, P074:5-22 one example not three, P074:25-27 Yusuf verse as a poem in prose, P074:29-33; 3:26 at P074:23-24 not repeated: in full in S27; B7.O.130, B7.O.131). [B7.O.130 PART: recall B1 S12 "an unanswered prayer protects the asker (EZ)", then new: the old dua for a good end against the wish for an unfit spouse, in EZ P073:1-28 | B7.O.131 NEW (verdict changed from PART, see Repeats)]
  4. Which duas never run out? Our wishes are limited (how much tea will one man drink?), but forgiveness and raised degrees for the believers have no limit, which is why the funeral prayer asks it; the relief (faraj) of the Imam of the Age is not yet granted, so it is asked without end, by him and by us; and salawat, asked any number of times, is still granted, because on that side there is no limit. (EZ H3 P075:1, P075:16-23 with block #20, P076:1-21, P076:22-25 as "salawat is a dua with no limit" only; AH re-read RR1-053 ZH-S33 ¶149-153 (forty days of weeping brought Moses forward by 170 years; pray for the Imam's relief), RR2-044 ZH-S32 ¶224-225 ("the matter is not in my hand", a dream report), RR2-011 EJ-S10 ¶178-182 (a request for one's brothers in faith is answered for certain); Teacher Copy: B7.H.36 (JS U7.21-4 P199 to P202, the two views on salawat), EZ P076:26-28 (4:85, «وتقبل شفاعته وارفع درجته»), RR2-043, RR5-023). [B7.O.132 NEW | B7.H.36 NEW, Teacher Copy | RR1-053, RR2-011 PART | RR2-044 NEW (AH carries this step) | RR1-054, RR3-014, RR4-021 SAME: recall only]
  5. What is praise, and why is all praise His? Hamd is praise of Him for what He is (all knowledge, all power, all life), shukr answers a gift, dhikr is wider than both; all praise is His because every perfection and every good is from Him and His Essence holds every perfection; and «الذي جعل الحمد مفتاحاً لذكره وسبباً للمزيد من فضله ودليلاً على آلائه وعظمته». (JS H2 P050:3-20, P051:4-13; JS U7.1-2 P063:3-9, P064:2-8; EZ U7.1-2 P066 madh, shukr, hamd; Nahj 157 at JS H2 P052:1-22 as a block; within the Bab: recall S02 "MM: tahmid proclaims that every perfection is His property" (U7.1-2 MM P069:3); Teacher Copy: B7.O.103 (JS H2 P053)). [B7.A.24 PART: recall B1 S03 "all praise is His alone" and B3 S08 "al-Fatiha's «الحمد لله رب العالمين» as the root of the school (JS)", then new: praise is His because every perfection is from Him (JS U7.1-2 P063:3-9, P064:2-8), and EZ's madh, shukr, hamd (P066) | B7.O.101 NEW | B7.O.102 NEW | B7.O.103 NEW, Teacher Copy]
  6. How does one reach great thanks? Al-Baqir: count little provision as much and much obedience as little, and reach great thanks through fear of losing blessings; one story (the healed hand) told without the lecturer's "I" and the living person. (JS H2 P054:3 one line, P054:7-22, block #15 extended in Jami wording, P054:46-109, P054:118-133; B7.O.104). [B7.O.104 NEW | AH's thanks records RR1-001, RR1-002, RR1-003, RR1-012, RR2-024, RR2-029, RR3-024 SAME: recall only]
  7. And while it is delayed, what is the servant's state? No complaint: no generous master is as patient with a base servant as He is with me; our fear and our hope are both from Him; and the servant's honour is to be His servant only, his pride that He is his Lord, «إلهي كفى بي عزاً أن أكون لك عبداً وكفى بي فخراً أن تكون لي رباً أنت كما أحب فاجعلني كما تحب»; since the Masumin speak from absolute knowledge, learn from their duas how to speak to Him. (EZ H3 P077:1-9 with block #21; JS H3 P057:4-13 without «استاد»; JS H3 P059 with P063 as block #18, starting at «إلهي»; JS H3 P062:1-5 as one bridge line; AH re-read RR1-051 ZH-S33 ¶118-124 (hope of relief keeps the heart alive); Teacher Copy: B7.O.134 (EZ H3 P078), B7.O.108 (JS H3 P058)). [B7.O.133 NEW | B7.O.107 NEW | B7.O.109 PART: recall B5 S04 "the munajat «كفى بي عزا ... أنت كما أحب فاجعلني كما تحب» as a tanzih note (JS)", then new: the honour of servanthood and pride in His lordship, in JS H3 P059:4-18, P063:2-16 | B7.O.111 PART: recall B1 S06 and B1 S11 "detailed knowledge of God comes through the infallibles (JS)", then new: how to speak to Him is learned only from them, JS H3 P062:1-5 | B7.O.134, B7.O.108 NEW, Teacher Copy | RR1-051 PART | RR1-059, RR3-034, RR3-035, RR3-036, RR3-037 SAME: recall only]
  8. Close: is contact itself the honour? Yes: whether by wird, dhikr or request, contact with absolute knowledge, absolute power and indescribable mercy is the servant's whole pride; to be allowed to speak with Him is an honour; so do not rise and run after prayer, sit and speak with Him. (JS H2 P056:4-12 with 25:77 as a one-line return (block in S27); JS H1 P033:3-9, P034; JS H3 P060:1-11 without the named scholar; Teacher Copy: B7.O.105 (JS H2 P055:9-14)). [B7.O.106 PART: recall B5 S04 "the division of wird, dhikr and du'a (JS)", then new: 25:77 as the ground that contact itself is the great honour, JS H2 P056:4-12 | B7.O.93 NEW | B7.O.110 NEW | B7.O.105 PART, Teacher Copy | RR3-013, RR4-020 SAME: recall only]

Who carries it

EZ carries steps 1 to 4 and the first half of step 7 (H3 P064 to P078); JS carries steps 5, 6, 8 and the second half of step 7 (H2 P050 to P056, H3 P057, P059, P060, P062, H1 P033).   • U10.13.7 (all praise is His because He is the root of every good, even a creature's chosen good; no one thanks Him as due; the beggar at Mina): Dropped: no AH or MM text; the framing to step 5 would be sound, but it repeats B7.A.24 (JS U7.1-2 P063:3-9) inside the Bab and is Bab 10's own claim.
  • U10.13.8 (Fatima opens the Fadak sermon with praise amid calamity): Dropped: no AH or MM text (EZ only); praise amid calamity could support step 7, but the step already has the Iftitah and AH's RR1-051, and the sermon's opening is in B1 S12.
  • U10.13.9 (He gives knowledge and any perfection where He wills; "from where is good for me, O Lord"): Dropped: no AH or MM text (EZ only); its claim is the source of perfections, Bab 10's and U7.1's, not any step here.
  • U10.13.10 (18:65, the righteous servant taught knowledge from Our presence): Dropped: no sound framing; God-given knowledge is not a step of this lesson; EZ only.
  • U10.13.11 (Mary chosen, 3:42; 1:7 completed in 5:3; 16:78 that you may give thanks; thanks for rain): Dropped: no sound framing as a set; only 16:78 touches thanks, and EZ's 16:78 is B3 S07; EZ only.
  • U10.13.12 (all creatures have one Creator but are not equal before God, 17:70): Dropped: no sound framing; no step asks about rank among creatures; EZ only.
  • U10.13.13 (healing placed in Husayn's soil as recompense): Dropped: no sound framing; read as an answer to a dua it would be poor judgement, like MM's passage on Husayn's rising framed as yaqin; EZ only.
  • U10.13.14 (49:17, do not count your Islam a favour to me): Dropped: no sound framing; the servant's honour in step 7 is servanthood itself, not guidance as a favour; EZ only.
  • U10.13.15 (the Book of God: one end in His hand, one in yours): Dropped: no sound framing; holding to the Quran is not contact by dua in the sense of step 8; EZ only.
  • U10.13.16 ("I do not acquit myself, except as my Lord has mercy", 12:53): Dropped: no sound framing; staying pure is not called one's own: closer to U7.42 or Bab 10 than to answer, praise or patience; EZ only.
  • U10.13.17 (Solomon to Bilqis' envoys, 27:36; "God comes between a man and his heart", 8:24): Dropped: no sound framing; neither verse carries a step; EZ only.
  • U10.13.18 (the Ghadir greeting, "praise to God who made us among those who hold to the wilaya"): Dropped: no sound framing; a praise formula for wilaya would only add a text to step 5, not support "why praise is His"; EZ only.
  • U10.13.19 (a bundle of about twenty JS and EZ points: the basmala, madh and hamd, ata' and hiba, unearned gifts ...): Dropped: no AH or MM text (held by JS and EZ only); its madh and hamd point repeats step 5's own JS and EZ lines.
  • U13.13/1 (supplications disclose His beauty or His majesty and so give intimacy or fear; AH, JS, EZ): kept, Teacher Copy at step 7, only once AH's passage is gathered (no text in the packet; the atlas line alone). Claude's judgement it supports step 7, whose JS line (H3 P057:6-9) says our fear and our hope are both from Him; duas that show His majesty give fear and those that show His beauty give hope, which also gives the reconciliation diag Q10 asks for between JS P057:6 and EZ 7.14.10 (fear His justice meeting our deeds, not Him). If the gathered AH words do not say this, drop.
  • U13.13/2 (remembrance until death, not the Sufis' silence and seclusion; AH MV-S12 ¶14-16): Dropped: no sound framing; AH's "no limit" is on how much dhikr one does, while step 4's "no limit" is on what a dua asks; and this same AH passage is already imported into B1 S17's Teacher Copy (IM-H17/P006).
  • U13.13/3 (take from God by insistence; AH, JS): kept, Teacher Copy at the end of step 3, only once AH's passage is gathered (no text in the packet). Claude's judgement it supports EZ's close of step 3 (H3 P074:29-33), "do not be hard on God and keep asking; every asking brings a reward", the same claim from the side of the asker. If S27's "never neglect asking" step takes it, it moves there.
Teacher Copy only

B7.K.30 whole under step 1, with 5:64, the refiner's omissions there (salam argument P113:5-7, the Bukhari report P113:18-21, the Wahhabi doubts P114:19-23; diag U7.24 Q11), «اللهم إني أتوجه إليك بنبيك» (fine layer); B7.H.42 (healing «وكل علة» "unless death is set") under step 1; B7.H.36 (JS's two views on salawat; JS P202:13 prefers the second, that our salawat raises their station, which is also EZ's reading at P076:22-28) and EZ P076:26-28 (4:85, «وتقبل شفاعته وارفع درجته») under step 4; B7.O.103 (praise to the Creator of night and day, JS H2 P053, no Arabic) under step 5; the second story of step 6 (the elder in Tehran, bread and cheese, JS H2 P054:24-49) and P054:94-95 (the doubtful variant «واستجد حقيقة الشكر», NOT-FOUND); B7.O.134 (the Persian tale of the spilled wheat and the purse, EZ H3 P078; JS's reading DZ_0047 that the man's al-hamdu lillah was a complaint) and B7.O.108 (the patience hadith, JS H3 P058) under step 7; B7.O.105 under step 8; JS H3 P057:14-19 (the man at the edge of Jahannam) only with its narration named (see Texts); RR2-043, RR5-023; every SAME record under its recall; the two kept imports. Lecture words cut: JS H2 P050:1-2, P051:1-2; «استاد» removed at H3 P057:1, P058:19, P058:26, P067:23, P067:32, P078:30, P078:42; «مرحوم علامہ مجلسی» and his father out of the Handout (P060:13).

Held out

no ledger point of this lesson is HELD. Diagnosis moves touching it, in the Teacher Copy flagged until Ali rules: JS H3 P058 to U7.12 or Teacher Copy (Q12); P062's claim to U7.2 (H03) beyond the bridge line (the U7.2 diagnosis does not take it); H2 P055:15-18 cut (Q15). The inbound list's other dua texts (U7.42 munajat, U7.12 H5, U7.41 O.46 to O.51, U7.41 EZ P044 «أيما ثلاثة مؤمنين اجتمعوا») are not pulled in: they are homed in their own lessons or S27.

Texts and Jami results 7
  1. «وَلَعَلَّ الَّذِي أَبْطَأَ عَنِّي هُوَ خَيْرٌ لِي لِعِلْمِكَ بِعَاقِبَةِ الْأُمُورِ» (Du'a al-Iftitah, EZ H3 P067:29, block #19): Jami FOUND-OTHER-WORDING against the print: Iqbal al-A'mal 1 «أبطأ عني ... بعاقبة الأمور», Maladh al-Akhyar 10; the Bihar 91 matn in the same part reads «أبطأ عليّ ... بعواقب الأمور»; the print's «ابتاعني» is a garble.
  2. «اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ وَالْمُؤْمِنَاتِ وَالْمُسْلِمِينَ وَالْمُسْلِمَاتِ الْأَحْيَاءِ مِنْهُمْ وَالْأَمْوَاتِ وَتَابِعْ بَيْنَنَا وَبَيْنَهُمْ بِالْخَيْرَاتِ» (third takbir of the funeral prayer, EZ H3 P075:19, block #20): FOUND, al-Hidaya 353, Misbah al-Mutahajjid 525-526.
  3. «الحمد لله الذي جعل الحمد مفتاحاً لذكره وسبباً للمزيد من فضله ودليلاً على آلائه وعظمته» (JS H2 P052:1-2): FOUND, Imam Ali, Nahj al-Balagha (Subhi al-Salih) 168, khutba 157; the lesson is right, the atlas line (al-Sajjad) is wrong.
  4. «وَتَخَلَّصْ إِلَى عَظِيمِ الشُّكْرِ بِاسْتِكْثَارِ قَلِيلِ الرِّزْقِ وَاسْتِقْلَالِ كَثِيرِ الطَّاعَةِ ... وَتَوَسَّلْ إِلَى عَظِيمِ الشُّكْرِ بِخَوْفِ زَوَالِ النِّعَمِ» (al-Baqir to Jabir, JS H2 P054:15, :59, :93): FOUND, Tuhaf al-'Uqul 286, Bihar 75:164; the print's «نعمة» for «النعم» and the split into two fragments are fixed from Jami.
  5. «فَلَمْ أَرَ مَوْلًى كَرِيماً أَصْبَرَ عَلَى عَبْدٍ لَئِيمٍ مِنْكَ عَلَيَّ» (Iftitah, EZ H3 P077:7, block #21): FOUND, Iqbal al-A'mal 1, Bihar 91:132.
  6. «إِلَهِي كَفَى بِي عِزّاً أَنْ أَكُونَ لَكَ عَبْداً وَكَفَى بِي فَخْراً أَنْ تَكُونَ لِي رَبّاً أَنْتَ كَمَا أُحِبُّ فَاجْعَلْنِي كَمَا تُحِبُّ» (Amir al-Mu'minin, JS H3 P059:1-2, block #18): FOUND, al-Khisal, Rawdat al-Wa'izin 255, Bihar 91:92; start the excerpt at «إلهي»; inline «کفا», «فجعلنی» to «كفى», «فاجعلني».
  7. «افعل بنا ما أنت أهله ولا تفعل بنا ما نحن أهله» (EZ H3 P073:27, inline): FOUND-OTHER-WORDING, Jami «وافعل بي وبفلان ما أنت أهله ولا تفعل بنا ما نحن أهله فإنك أهل التقوى وأهل المغفرة» (sajdat al-shukr, Ikhtiyar al-Misbah 120; Miftah al-Falah 269). Others: «إنك تدعوني فأولي عنك» FOUND (Iqbal 1); «وتقبل شفاعته وارفع درجته» FOUND (al-Sadiq's tashahhud, al-Tahdhib); the patience hadith FOUND as a qudsi hadith in Bihar 93:252 (not vol. 96), and Jami's own text ends «والصبر الصوم»; JS H3 P057:14-19 is al-Baqir's report in al-Mahasin 1:94 (Bihar 67:387), which ends in forgiveness; JS H1 P033:7 ("had You not permitted us") is nearest Munajat al-Dhakirin «وإذنك لنا بدعائك»; RR2-044's «ليس الأمر بيدي» is a dream report (Bihar 53:332-333, the Sayyid named Haydar there, Halawi in AH).
Next lesson picks up

S28 is the last lesson of Bab 7; the hand on is Bab 8's first question, written when Bab 8 is planned. Inside the Bab it closes on the servant's honour in contact with Him, the thread S27 opened («دعا کیا ہے اور کس سے مانگیں؟»).

How this draft was made

What this draft does (the rules applied)
  1. SAME (already taught in Bab 1 to 6): not taught again; one recall line naming the earlier lesson; the scholar's repeat sits under it in the Teacher Copy as a flagged ask.
  2. PART: the shared base recalled in one line, then the new part taught in full in the scholar's own new wording.
  3. Core repeat: kept in full and flagged (core repeat · ask).
  4. Arabic printed in full in an earlier Bab: printed again, flagged with that lesson.
  5. Imports: thin lessons only, never from an earlier Bab, kept only with a sound framing marked as Claude's judgement.
  6. Standing: Claude keeps nothing out (every cut, move or doubt is an Ask; held points stay in the Teacher Copy, flagged); AH and MM carry, EZ and JS add or confirm; Teacher Copy only; no cross-reference to other courses.
